māttiraikku imai, noṭi īraḷavu kōṭal -
kaṭpulaṉākiya imaikkālamum, cevippulaṉākiya noṭik kālamum karutik kōṭaṟku iraṇṭu ōtiṉār.			(naṉ. 99 mayilai.)

mār, al, ai vikutikaḷ -
‘kāṇaṉmār emar’ eṉa mārīṟu etirmaṟai viyaṅkō ḷiṭattum, ṭu tu ṟu - eṉpaṉa oṉṟaṉ paṭarkkaiyiṭattum, alvikuti ‘makaṉeṉal makkaṭ pataṭieṉal’ (kuṟaḷ 196) eṉa viyaṅkōḷ etirmaṟaiyilum uṭaṉpāṭṭilum, ai īṟu ‘añcāmai añcuva toṉṟiṉ’ eṉa viyaṅkōḷ etirmaṟaiyilum varum.	(naṉ. 140 irāmā.)
‘māṉam illai’ -
ittoṭar tolkāppiyattil payila vaḻaṅkukiṟatu ‘māṉam’ eṉṟa iccol vaḻaṅkappaṭumiṭattu muṉmoḻi perum pāṉmaiyum makaramey īṟṟatāy uḷḷatu. cantiyil makaram keṭa ‘mikiṉu māṉa millai’ eṉṟē toṭar amaikiṟatu. māṉam eṉṟum āṉam eṉṟum appuṇarmoḻiyil collaip pirikkalām. iṉampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiya rum māṉam eṉpatē collākakkoṇṭu ‘māṉamillai’ eṉṟa toṭarkkuk kuṟṟamillai’ eṉṟu poruḷ ceytaṉar.		(to.e. 199, 271 nac. mutaliyaṉa.)
‘āṉam’ eṉpatē collākak koṇṭu (āṉam - hāṉam) ‘kuṟṟam’ eṉṟu karuttuk koḷvār cilar. (e.ku.pak.190) āyiṉ, to.col. 111āmnūṟpāvil (cēṉā.) ippirippup poruntāmai kāṇka.
māṉam illai - varaivu (nīkkutal) illai - eṉpar cilar.
māṉam kuṟṟam eṉṟa poruḷil ‘māṉamillai maṟṟavaṉ māṭṭeṉa’ peruṅkatai -ai : 47: 225 eṉṟa toṭaril vantuḷḷatu.
mikkup puṇarum puṇarcciyiṉ iruvakai -
mikkup puṇarum puṇarcci, eḻuttu mikutalum, cāriyai mikutalum eṉa iru vakaittu.
	e-ṭu :	viḷa+kōṭu = viḷaṅkōṭu - ṅakaramey mikkatu.
			maka+ kai = makaviṉkai - iṉcāriyai mikkatu.  
(to.e. 112 nac.)
mikaṟkai -
mikaṟkai - mikutal; iyalpākap puṇara vēṇṭiya iṭattil valleḻuttu mikutalākiya nilai tōṉṟutal.
tāy eṉpatu vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi iyalpākap puṇarum eṉṟavitikku māṟāka iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tokaiyil,  tāy+ kolai = tāyk kolai - eṉṟu mikutalai ‘mikaṟkai’ eṉṟār.	(to.e.157. nac.)
miṉ piṉ paṉ kaṉ:puṇarcci -
miṉ mutaliya innāṉku coṟkaḷum, toḻiṟpeyar pōla, yakaram nīṅkiya piṟameykaḷ varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ variṉ ukarac cāriyai peṟum. kaṉ eṉpatoṉṟum akaraccāriyai peṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttum iṉamelleḻuttum mikku uṟaḻum.
	e-ṭu:	miṉṉuk kaṭitu, miṉṉu nīṇṭatu, miṉṉu valitu; miṉṉuk kaṭumai, miṉṉunīṭci, miṉṉuvalimai - eṉa alvaḻi vēṟṟumai iruvaḻiyum ukaraccāriyai peṟṟatu. ēṉaiya mūṉṟoṭum ivvāṟē porunta ōṭṭuka. kaṉṉuk kaṭitu, kaṉṉuk kaṭumai - mēṟkūṟiya potuvāṉa muṭipu. kaṉṉat taṭṭu, kaṉṉan taṭṭu - ‘kaṉ’ akaram peṟṟu melliṉattōṭu  uṟaḻntatu.
			(kaṉ - ciṟutarācut taṭṭu)	(naṉ. 217)
miṉ: puṇarumāṟu -
miṉ eṉṟa col miṉṉutal toḻilaiyum miṉṉalaiyum kuṟikkum. iḵtu alvaḻiyilum vēṟṟumaiyilum ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum, yakaram varuvaḻi iyalpum, uyir varuvaḻi īṟṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum peṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	miṉṉuk kaṭitu; miṉṉu naṉṟu,  miṉṉu valitu, miṉ yātu; miṉṉaritu -alvaḻi. miṉṉukkaṭumai; miṉṉu naṉmai, miṉyāppu, miṉṉuvalimai; miṉṉarumai - vēṟṟumai	(to.e.345 nac.)
‘mī eṉa marīiya iṭam varai kiḷavi’ -
mī eṉpatu iyalpāṉa col aṉṟu; mēṟku eṉṟa col mī eṉa   marīiyiṟṟu eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
mēl eṉṟa col īṟṟumey keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāka maruvutal iyalpu ātaliṉ, mēl eṉpatu mī eṉa maruviṟṟu eṉpatu ciṟakkum.	(to.po.pak. 742 pērā.)
iṭam varaital - mēliṭattai varaiyaṟuttuc cuṭṭutal.			(e.ā.pak. 142 to.e. 250 nac.)
mīkaṇ, mīkkūṟṟu, mīkkōḷ, mīntōl col muṭivu -
mīkaṇ eṉpatu kaṇṇiṉatu mēliṭam eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟkumēṉum, āṟaṉurupiṉ payaṉilaiyām mī eṉṉum varumoḻi nilaimoḻiyāy niṉṟu valleḻuttu mikātu puṇarntamaiyiṉ ilakkaṇap pōliyāy alvaḻiyāyiṟṟu.
mīkkūṟṟu eṉpatu pukaḻ. atu mēlāya collāṉ piṟanta pukaḻ eṉṉum mēmpāṭu eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ, paṇput tokaip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻittokai.
mīkkōḷ eṉpatu mēṟpōrvai. atu yākkaiyiṉmēl koḷḷutalaiyuṭaiya pōrvai - eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumaittokaip puṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokai. īṇṭu mī eṉṟatu iṭapporuḷōṭu ēḻām vēṟṟumaiyurupiṉ poruḷ paṭa niṉṟatēṉum, kaṇāti urupu vēṇṭāmaiyiṉ, vēṟṟumait tokai yāyiṟṟu.
mīntōl eṉpatu mēṟṟōl. atu mēlāya tōl - eṉap poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ paṇputtokai. iḵtu ik kālattup pīntōl eṉa marīiyiṟṟu.	(naṉ. 178 caṅkara.)
mī : puṇarumāṟu -
mī eṉpatu mēl eṉṟa colliṉ marūu. atu varumoḻi vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyalpākavum, varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkum, varumoḻi valleḻuttukku iṉamāṉa mel leḻuttup peṟṟum puṇarum. iḵtu alvaḻi muṭivu.		 	(e.ā. pak. 142)
	e-ṭu :	mīkaṇ, mīcevi, mītalai, mīpuṟam;mīkkōḷ, mīppal;
			mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl - mēlākiya kaṇ mutalākap poruḷ ceyka.
mīkaṇ eṉpataṟku mēliṭattukkaṇ eṉṟu vēṟṟumaip poruḷpaṭa urai kūṟuvar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉi yarum. kaṇ eṉpataṉai iṭappeyarākak koṇṭu mī ākiya kaṇ - mēliṭam- eṉṟu poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar.			(e.ku.pak. 229)
mīṉ puṇarumāṟu -
mīṉ eṉṟa col alvaḻip puṇarcciyil vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum; vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarckikkaṇ valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu	:	mīṉ karitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu : alvaḻi; mīṉkaṇ, mīṟkaṇ; mīṉciṉai, mīṟciṉai; mīṉṟalai, mīṟṟalai; mīṉpuṟam, mīṟpuṟam - vēṟṟumai uṟaḻcci muṭipu.				(to.e.339 nac.)
‘mīṉ’ vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇartal -
mīṉ eṉṉum nilaimoḻi īṟṟu ṉakaram vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattē ṟakarattōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum; alvaḻikkaṇ  iyalpupuṇarcciyām.
	e-ṭu	:	mīṉ + kaṇ =mīṟkaṇ, mīṉkaṇ; mīṉ +cevi = mīṟcevi, mīṉcevi; mīṟṟalai, mīṉṟalai;mīṟpuṟam, mīṉpuṟam - eṉpaṉavum kāṇka.
			mīṉ kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu - eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu.	(naṉ. 213)
muṭiviṭam kūṟal -
āciriyaṉ tāṉ collum ilakkaṇattiṟku vitiyuḷḷa iṭattaic collutal muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉṉum utti. ‘laḷaīṟṟu iyaipiṉām āytam aḵkum’ eṉṉum ic cūttiram, ‘kuṟilvaḻi laḷa-ttav vaṇaiyiṉ āytam, ākavum peṟūum alvaḻi yāṉē’ (naṉ. 228) eṉṉum cūttirattai nōkkik kūṟaliṉ, muṭiviṭaṅkūṟal eṉṉum uttiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.			(naṉ. 97 caṅkara.)
muttuvīriya eḻuttatikārac ceytikaḷ -
innūl eḻuttatikāram eḻuttiyal, moḻiyiyal, puṇariyal- eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. atikārat taṟciṟappup pāyiram nīṅkalāka ivaṟṟil muṟaiyē 115, 45, 298 āka 458 nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. eḻuttiyalil naṉṉūliṉ eḻuttiyaṟ ceyti curukkiyum virittum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. eḻuttu, uyirmey, mayakkam- mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pariyāyap peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. aḷapeṭai eṭṭuvakaippaṭum eṉak kaṇakkiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. moḻiyiyaliṉ toṭakkattil ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷ, peyar viṉai eṉṟa pakuppu, moḻi eḻu vakaippaṭum eṉṟa piṟarkūṟṟu - eṉpaṉa kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷa.  vaṭamoḻi eḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻil tirintu vaḻaṅkumāṟum vaṭamoḻit  tīrkka kuṇa virutti cantikaḷum, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻil tirintu vaḻaṅkumāṟum virittup pēcappaṭṭuḷa. puṇariyalil, potup puṇarcci - urupu puṇarcci - uyirīṟu meyyīṟu kuṟṟiyalukaraīṟu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ puṇarcci- yāvum naṉṉūlaiyum tolkāppiyattaiyum oṭṭik kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. kō-mā- eṉpaṉa yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟutal, eṇṇup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkumiṭattu iṉcāriyai peṟutal, ḻakaram takaram varuvaḻi ṭakara mātal, ḻakaramāṉatu nakaram varuvaḻi ṇakaramātal - mutaliyaṉa putiyaṉa pukutalāka ippuṇariyaluḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷa.
muttuvīriya  eḻuttiyal cuṭṭum pariyāyap peyarkaḷ -
eḻuttu	-	irēkai, vari, poṟi- eṉpaṉa 					(nuṟpāeṇ3)
uyir	-	accu, āvi, curam,pūtam - eṉpaṉa (7)
kuṟṟeḻuttu	-	kuṟumai, iraccuvam, kuṟil - eṉpaṉa (9)
neṭṭeḻuttu	-	neṭumai, tīrkkam, neṭil - eṉpaṉa (11)
mey	-	ūmai, uṭal, oṟṟu, kāttiram - eṉpaṉa (13)
valleḻuttu	-	vaṉmai, vaṉkaṇam, vali- eṉpaṉa (15)
melleḻuttu	-	meṉmai, meṉkaṇam, meli- eṉpaṉa (17)
iṭaiyeḻuttu	-	iṭaimai, iṭaikkaṇam, iṭai-eṉpaṉa 				(19)
cārpu	-	pullal, cārtal,puṇartal eṉpaṉa (23)
āytam	-	 aḵkēṉam, taṉinilai - eṉpaṉa (28)
cuṭṭu	-	kāṭṭal, kuṟittal - eṉpaṉa (29)
viṉā	-	viṉaval, kaṭāval - eṉpaṉa (30)
aḷapeṭai	-	aḷapu, pulutam- eṉpaṉa (33)
iṭainilai 		caṅkam, puṇarcci, caiyōkam, pullal,
mey mayakkam	ṉū	kalattal, mayakkam, iṭainilai eṉpaṉa (66)	māttirai	-	maṭṭu, aḷavu, mitam, varai - eṉpaṉa (97)
muttuvīriya eḻuttiyaluḷ kāṇappaṭum vicēṭak kuṟippukkaḷ cila -
melleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭam talai (eḻut. 43). eṭuttalum paṭuttalum eṉa ōcai iruvakaittu (59). nakaram ‘porun’ eṉa oru coṟkaṇṇēyē iṟutiyāka varum (90). kainnoṭi yākiya māttirai uṉṉukiṟa kālattuk kāl māttirai, viralai ūṉṟukiṉṟa kālattu arai, viralkaḷai muṟukkukiṟa kālattu mukkāl, muṟukkiyavaṟṟai ōcaippaṭa viṭukkiṟa kālattu oṉṟu- eṉa muṟaiyē amaiyumāṟu (98)- iṉṉōraṉṉa ceytikaḷ piṟa ilakkaṇa nūlil kāṇappaṭāvāy in nūṟkē vicēṭamāṉa kuṟippukkaḷām.
muttuvīriyap puṇariyaluḷ kāṇappaṭum cila ciṟappuc ceytikaḷ -
taḻāt toṭarkaḷai ‘mayaṅkiyal moḻi’ eṉṟu kuṟikkum mu.vī. tolkāppiya nūṟpāvai oṭṭiyatu. avaiyum nilai varumoḻikaḷāka niṉṟu puṇartaṟkuriyaṉa.
	e-ṭu: teyva māl varai’ (‘māl teyva varai’ eṉka.)(5)
	ā,mā,kō- eṉṟu peyarkaḷ iṉcāriyai peṟutalumām.
	e-ṭu : 	āviṉai, māviṉai, kōviṉai	(16)
	kō,mā, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ yakara uṭampaṭumey varutalumām.
varumāṟu: kōyil, māyiru ñālam	 (25)
eṇkaḷ ellām iṉcāriyai peṟum.
	e-ṭu :	oṉṟiṉai, nāṉkiṉai	(69)
	nilā eṉṉum peyar iṉcāriyai peṟum. (tol. kūṟuvatu vēṟu. 228 nac.) varumāṟu.: nilāviṉ kānti	(93)
irā eṉṉum peyarkku iṉcāriyai illai (tol. kūṟuvatu vēṟu. 227. nac.)	(94)
ḻakaram vēṟṟumaikkaṇ takara nakaraṅkaḷ varumiṭattē, muṟaiyē ṭakara ṇakaramākat tiriyum. (ippuṇarppu vīracōḻiyattuḷ kaṇṭatu)
	e-ṭu :kīḻ+ ticai = kīṭṭicai; cōḻa + nāṭu = cōṇāṭu (210, 211)
muttuvīriyam -
19ām nuṟṟāṇṭil muttuvīra upāttiyāyar eṉpavarāl, tolkāppiyam naṉṉūl mutaliyavaṟṟil ariyavākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷai eḷimaippaṭuttik kūṟuvataṟkāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa aintilakkaṇa nul muttuvīriyam. iḵtu eḻuttu col poruḷ yāppu aṇi - eṉṉum aimpirivukaḷai yuṭaiyatu. avaṟṟuḷ eḻuttatikāram, eḻuttiyal moḻi yiyal puṇariyal - eṉṉum muppakuppiṉatu. collatikāram, peyariyal viṉaiyiyal oḻipiyal- eṉṉum muppiriviṟṟu. poruḷatikāram, akavoḻukka iyal kaḷavoḻukka iyal kaṟpoḻukka iyal - eṉṉum muppālatu. yāppatikāram, uṟuppiyal ceyyuḷiyal oḻipiyal eṉṟa mūviyaliṉatu. iṉi eñciya aṇiyatikāram, collaṇiyiyal poruḷaṇiyiyal ceyyuḷaṇiyiyal - eṉṟa mūṉṟu iyalkaḷāṉ iyaṉṟatu. innūṟku urai varaintavar tirunelvēlit tiruppāṟkaṭal nātaṉ kavirāyar eṉṟu karutukiṟōm. aintu atikāraṅ kaḷilum muṟaiyē 458, 310, 92, 266, 159, āka 1285 nuṟpākkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aintu atikāraṅkaḷilum uḷḷa taṟciṟappup pāyira nuṟpākkaḷaiyum kūṭṭa 1290nuṟpākkaḷām.
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum aḷapeṭai vakaikaḷ -
iyaṟkaiyaḷapeṭai	-	aḻaittal, vilaikūṟal, 					pulampal - ivaṟṟuḷ varuvatu
ceyaṟkai 	-	ceyyuḷil cīrtaḷai keṭṭa 					viṭattup pulavaṉ koḷvatu.
iṉṉicai yaḷapeṭai	-	‘keṭuppatūum’ 							eṭuppatūum’ (kuṟaḷ 15)
collicai yaḷapeṭai	-	taḷaii (taḷaintu eṉpatu 					tirintu aḷapeṭuttatu.)
neṭilaḷapeṭai	-	taṉi neṭṭeḻuttu 						aḷapeṭuppatu : āa
kuṟilaḷapeṭai	-	kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭilāki 					aḷapeṭuppatu:
		paḻūup pal
oṟṟaḷapeṭai	-	kaṇṇ
eḻuttuppēṟaḷapeṭai	-	uvāap patiṉāṉku

muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum tattitam paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ -
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum ikara ēkāraṅkaḷ aikāram ākum.
	e-ṭu :	kiriyil uḷḷaṉa kairikam: vērattāl varuvatu vairam (vēram - kōpam; vairam - pakaimai)
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum ukara ūkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ oḷakāram ākum. e-ṭu: kurukulattil vantavar kauravar; cūraṉmakaṉ cauri; cōmaṉ putalvaṉ caumiyaṉ.
moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṟkum akaram ākāram ākum. e-ṭu: caṉakaṉ makaḷ cāṉaki; tacarataṉ putalvaṉ tācarati.	(moḻi. 43 - 45)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum ‘pēcuṅ kiḷavi’ -
‘pēcuṅ kiḷavi’ yākiya vaḻaṅkum col caṅkatam, pākatam, caṉukkirakam, avappirañcaṉam - eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. in nāṉkaṉuḷ, caṅkatamum caṉukkirakamum tēvarmoḻi yākum. avappirañcaṉam eṉpatu iḻintōratu moḻi. ēṉaiya pākatam eṉpatu nāṭukaḷellām vaḻaṅkum moḻi.
pākatam taṟpavam eṉavum, taṟcamam eṉavum, tēcikam eṉavum mūvakaippaṭum.  āriyac ciṟappeḻuttāṉum, potuvum ciṟappumākiya iraṇṭeḻuttāṉum tamiḻil citaintu varuvatu taṟpavamām. e-ṭu : cuki, pōki; araṉ, ari.
āriyattiṟkum tamiḻiṟkum potuveḻuttālākiya moḻi taṟcamamām. e-ṭu : amalam, kāraṇam, kamalam.
tēcikam eṉpatu ticaiccol ākum. e-ṭu: tāyait taḷḷai eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, tantaiyai accaṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum kāṇka.	(moḻi. 26-33)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum moḻiyiṭai eḻutteṇṇikkai -
moḻi ōreḻuttu mutalāka oṉpatu eḻuttu iṟutiyākat toṭarntu varum. (pakupatam, pakāppatam - eṉa naṉṉūl patattaip pākupaṭuttiyamai pōla mu.vī. pākupaṭuttilatu-)
e-ṭu: kā, aṇi, aṟam, akalam, aruppam, ampalavaṉ, araṅkattāṉ, uttarāṭattāṉ, uttaraṭṭāti yāṉ - eṉa muṟaiyē kāṇka.	(moḻi. 9)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum moḻivakai -
muttuvīriyam ēḻ moḻivakaikaḷaic cuṭṭukiṟatu. avai varumāṟu:
nilam, nīr 	-	pirikkappaṭāta taṉimoḻi
tēraṉ, ūraṉ	-	pirikkappaṭum iṇaimoḻi
aracar vantār	-	toṭarntu varum tuṇaimoḻi
naṅkai, vēṅkai	-	taṉimoḻiyum,(nam+kai, vēm+kai 				-eṉat) toṭarmoḻiyum ākum 					potumoḻi
cantiraṉ	-	orumaiyaik kāṭṭum taṇamoḻi
muṉivarkaḷ	-	paṉmaiyaik kāṭṭum kaṇamoḻi
āṇ, peṇ	-	ivai irutiṇaiyilum kalantu 					potuvāka  varum kalappuṟu moḻi				(moḻi. 8)
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum vaṭamoḻic cantikaḷ -
muttuvīriyam, tīrkkacanti kuṇacanti virutticanti- ākiya mūvakai vaṭamoḻic cantikaḷaik kuṟikkiṟatu. nilaimoḻi yum varumoḻiyum vaṭacollāka amaiyumiṭattē ivai nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
a)	nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalilum a ā varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa,iṭaiyilōr ākāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu :pata + ampuyam = patāmpuyam; cēnā + atipati =  cēnātipati
ā)		nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil i ī niṟka varumoḻi mutalilum i ī  varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyil ōr īkāram tōṉṟum,
	e-ṭu : maki + intiraṉ = makīntiraṉ; purī +  īcaṉ = purīcaṉ.
i)	nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil u ū niṟka, varumoḻi mutalilum uū varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyil ōr ūkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu: kuru + utayam =  kurūtayam; cuyampū +  ūrccitam = cuyampūrccitam. 
ivai mūṉṟum tīrkka cantiyām.
a)		nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil i ī varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyē ōr ēkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu:	nara + intiraṉ = narēntiraṉ; umā + īcaṉ = umēcaṉ
ā)		nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil u ū varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyē ōr ōkāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu:	tāma + utaraṉ = tāmōtaraṉ; kaṅkā+ ūrmi = kaṅkōrmi.
ivai iraṇṭum kuṇacantiyām.
a)		nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil ē ai varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyē ōr aikāram tōṉṟum.
e-ṭu:	civa + ēkanātaṉ = civaikanātaṉ; makā + aicu variyam = makaicuvariyam.
ā)		nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil a ā niṟka varumoḻi mutalil ō oḷa varumāyiṉ, īṟum mutalum ākiya avviraṇṭum keṭa, iṭaiyē ōr oḷakāram tōṉṟum.
	e-ṭu : kalaca + ōtaṉam = kalacautaṉam; makā + oḷaṭatam = makauṭatam.	(moḻi. 34-41)
ivai iraṇṭum virutticantiyām.
muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum vaṭamoḻiyākkam -
āriya uyireḻuttup patiṉāṟaṉuḷ naṭuvil niṟkum nāṉkum iṟutiyil niṟkum iraṇṭum allāta ēṉaiya paṉṉiraṇṭum, āriya meyyeḻuttu muppattēḻaṉuḷ kakaram mutal aintu varukkaṅkaḷiṉ mutalil niṟkum ka ca ṭa ta pa - eṉpaṉavum avaṟṟiṉ iṟutiyil niṟkum ṅa ña ṇa na ma - eṉpaṉavum ya ra la va ḷa - eṉpaṉavum ākiya patiṉaintum āriyam tamiḻ eṉṉum irumoḻikkum potuveḻuttukkaḷām. iṉi, āriyat tiṟkē ciṟappāṉa eḻuttuḷ, mēṟakūṟiyavāṟu iṭai yilum iṟutiyilum niṟkum uyir āṟum, potu nīṅkalāka eñciya mey irupattiraṇṭum iṭam peṟum.
āriyattiṟkuriya ciṟappeḻuttukkaḷ tamiḻil vaṭacollāku miṭattē tiriyap peṟum. avai tiriyumāṟu:
	8ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭaiyil ya eṉavum tiriyum. 30ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭaiyil ya eṉavum tiriyum. 31ām mey yeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭaiyil ṭa eṉavum tiriyum. 32ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ca eṉavum iṭaiyil ta eṉavum tiriyum. 33ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil a eṉavum iṭaiyil ka eṉavum tiriyum. 35ām meyyeḻuttu, col mutalil ka eṉavum iṭaiyil kk eṉavum tiriyum.			(moḻi. 11 - 20)
ākāra  iṟutip peyar aikāramākavum, īkāra iṟutippeyar ikaramākavum tiriyap peṟum. e-ṭu :	mālā- mālai; purī - puri.
piṟavum naṉṉūlāraip piṉpaṟṟiyē moḻintuḷḷavāṟu kāṇka.	(moḻi. 23-26)
tattitam paṟṟiya kuṟippu ‘muttuvīriyam cuṭṭum tattitam’ eṉpataṉkaṇ kāṇka.
mutal īreṇṇiṉmuṉ uyir varuṅkāl puṇarcci -
oṉṟu iraṇṭu- eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutal moḻi variṉ, oṉṟu iraṇṭu- eṉpaṉa oru iru- eṉat tirintu niṉṟa nilaiyil ukaram keṭa, or ir - eṉṟāki, mutal nīṇṭu ōr īr eṉat tirintu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarum.
	e-ṭu:	oṉṟu + akal = ōrakal, oṉṟu + uḻakku = ōruḻakku: iraṇṭu +akal= īrakal, iraṇṭu+ uḻakku= īruḻakku	(to.e.455nac.)
mutal eḻuttu -
moḻi tōṉṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa aṭippaṭai eḻuttuk kaḷākiya uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppatu eḻuttukkaḷ mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum. iṉi, orucolliṉ mutalil varum eḻuttu mutaleḻuttu eṉavum, mutaṉilai eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
e-ṭu :	‘cuṭṭumutal ākiya ikara iṟuti’ - atōḷi 				(to.e.159 nac.)
	‘ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟuti’ -etōḷi 	(e. 159)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal ukaram’ - atu 	(e. 176)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal ākiya ai eṉ iṟuti’ - avai 	(e. 177)
	‘cuṭṭu mutal vakaram’ - av 	(e. 183)
	‘mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalum urittē’ - mūvuḻakku (e. 457)
	‘āṟueṉ kiḷavi mutal nī ṭummē’ - āṟakal 	(e. 458)
	‘akam eṉ kiḷavikku... mutalnilai oḻiya’ - aṅkai 				(e. 315)
mutal, cārpu: tokai vakai viri -
mutaleḻuttiṟku ‘mutal’ eṉṟatu tokaiyākavum, ‘uyirum uṭampum’ eṉṟatu vakaiyākavum, ‘muppatu’ eṉṟatuviriyāka vum; cārpeḻuttiṟkuc ‘cārpu’ eṉṟatu tokaiyākavum. ‘pattum’ eṉṟatu vakaiyākavum, ‘oṉṟoḻi munnūṟṟeḻu pāṉ’ eṉṟatu viriyākavum;
ivvirutiṟattu eḻuttiṟkum ‘eḻuttu’ eṉṟatu tokai yākavum, ‘mutalcārpu’ eṉṟatu vakaiyākavum, ivviru tiṟattu eḻuttiṉ viriyum kūṭṭi ‘oṉṟoḻi nāṉūṟu’ eṉpatu viriyākavum koḷka.	(naṉ. 61. irāmā.)


mutalnilai viṉaippeyar -
tirai nurai alai taḷir - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa viṉaimutaṟ poruṇmai yuṇarttum ikaravikuti puṇarntu keṭṭu, mutaṉilai māttiraiyāy niṉṟu viṉaippeyar ātal paṟṟi, mutaṉilai viṉaippeyar- mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar - eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum.(cū.vi.pak. 33)
mutal, vaḻi, cārpu: viḷakkam -
mutaṉūlukku vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum oruvaṟku maintaṉum marumāṉum pōlum eṉka. oru nūl taṉakku vaḻinūlai nōkkiṉ mutaṉūlākavum, mutaṉūlai nōkkiṉ vaḻinūlāka vum, ayalnūlai nōkkac cārpunūlākavum niṟkum. eṉavē, mutaṉūl māttiraiyāy niṟpatu iṟaivaṉnūlum, vaḻinūl māttiraiyāy niṟpatu iṟuti nūlum aṉṟi, iṭainiṟkum nūlkaḷ ellām oruvaṟku maintaṉāyiṉāṉ maṟṟavaṟkut tantai āyiṉāṟpōla, mutaṉūlāyum vaḻinūlāyum niṟkum.		(naṉ. 8 caṅkara.)
‘mutalā ēṉa tampeyar mutalu’tal -
uyiroṭu cērntu moḻikku mutalākum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭap paṭṭaṉa allāta, moḻikku mutalākāta meykaḷum tammaik kuṟippiṭumiṭattu moḻikku mutalil varum. moḻikku mutalākāta meykaḷ ṅ ṭ ṇr l ḻ ḷ ṟ ṉ - eṉṟa oṉpatum ām.
	varumāṟu:	‘laṉaeṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar’ (to.e. 149 nac.); ‘ṭakāram ākum’(e. 302); ‘ṇakāra iṟuti (e. 302); ‘ṉakāra iṟuti’ - (e. 332); ‘ḷakāra iṟuti’ (e. 396) ‘rakāra ḻakāramāy’ (e. 95 nac.); ‘ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ’ (e. 66, 94)
mutaleḻuttiṉ tokai vakai viri -
mutaleḻuttu - eṉat tokaiyāṉ oṉṟum, uyireḻuttu uṭampeḻuttu - eṉa vakaiyāṉ iraṇṭum, uyir paṉṉiraṇ ṭum uṭampu patiṉeṭṭum eṉa viriyāṉ muppatum ām mutaleḻuttu.	(naṉ. 58 mayilai.)
mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭappiṟappum muyaṟcippiṟappum -
uyirum iṭaiyiṉa meyyum miṭaṟum, melliṉamey mūkkum, valliṉam mārpum iṭamākak koṇṭu piṟakkum. iḵtu iṭap piṟappu. muyaṟcippiṟappu varumāṟu:
a, ā - aṅkāttalāṉum, i, ī, e, ē, ai - aṅkāppoṭu, mēlvāyppalaṭiyai nāviḷimpu uṟutalāṉum, u., ū, o, ō, oḷa - aṅkāppōṭu, itaḻ kuvitalāṉum piṟakkum. (aṅkāppiṉaip piṟavaṟṟukkum koḷka.)
k, ṅ - nāviṉaṭi mēlvāyaṭiyai uṟutalāṉum, c,ñ - nāviṉ naṭu mēlvāynaṭuvai uṟutalāṉum, ṭ, ṇ - nāviṉ nuṉi mēlvāynuṉiyai uṟutalāṉum, t, n - muṉvāyppal aṭiyai nānuṉi uṟutalāṉum, p, m - kīḻmēl utaṭukaḷ uṟutalāṉum, y - nāviṉaṭi mēlvāyiṉ aṭiyai naṉṟāka uṟutalāṉum, r, ḻ - nāviṉ nuṉi mēlvāyai varuṭutalāṉum, l - muṉvāyppal aṭiyai nāviḷimpu vīṅki oṟṟutalāṉum, ḷ - mēlvāyai nāviḷimpu vīṅki varuṭutalāṉum, v - kīḻutaṭu mēṟpallai uṟutalāṉum, ṟ, ṉ - mēlvāyai nāviṉ nuṉi mika uṟutalāṉum piṟakkum.
(uṟutal - poruntutal; varuṭutal - taṭavutal; oṟṟutal - taṭṭutal)	(naṉ. 75 - 86)
mutaleḻuttu, cārpeḻuttu : peyarkkāraṇam -
tāmē tamittu niṟkaiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu eṉṟāyiṉa. avaiyē tammoṭu tām cārntum, iṭam cārntum, iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum cārntum vikārattāṉ varutaliṉ cārpeḻuttu eṉṟāyiṉa.	(naṉ.60 mayilai.)
mutaṟpōli cila -
‘maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu’ - ‘maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu’. ‘poṉyātta tār’ - ‘poṉñātta tār’ eṉa ‘yā’niṉṟa iṭattu ‘ñā’ niṟpiṉum amaiyum eṉṟār tolkāppiyaṉār.
‘ṇaṉaeṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
viṉaiyō raṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar’	(to.e.146 nac.)			(naṉ. 124 irāmā.)
mutaṉilai iṟutinilai iṭainilaikaḷukkup puṟaṉaṭai -
eḻuttukkaḷ tam peyaraic colli nilaimoḻi varumoḻi kaḷāyp puṇarumiṭattē, tamakkuk kūṟappaṭṭa vitiyaik kaṭantu, moḻi mutalākā eḻuttukkaḷ mutalākiyum moḻi yiṭai mayaṅka lākā eḻuttukkaḷ mayaṅkiyum iyalum.
	e-ṭu:‘avaṟṟuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavum tōṉṟum’
(to. e. 24 nac.)
itaṉkaṇ, taṉ peyarai moḻitaliṉ lakaram moḻimuta lāyum ‘avaṟṟuḷ’ eṉpataṉ īṟṟu ḷakarattoṭu mayaṅkiyum vantavāṟu.
‘kep peritu’ eṉpuḻi’ eṉpuḻi, ‘ke’ taṉ peyar moḻi taliṉ ‘ekaram meyyōṭu ēlātu’ eṉṟa viti yiṟantu meyyōṭu īṟāy niṉṟavāṟu.	(naṉ. 121)
mutaṉilai kālam kāṭṭal -
toṭṭāṉ, viṭṭāṉ, uṟṟāṉ, peṟṟāṉ, pukkāṉ, naṭṭāṉ- eṉpaṉavaṟṟuḷ iṭainilai iṉṟi mutaṉilai vikāramāy iṟanta kālam kāṭṭiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ muṟaiyē toṭu viṭu uṟu peṟu puku naku - eṉpaṉa mutaṉilaikaḷ.			(naṉ. 145 caṅkara.)
mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar, ēval viṉai puṇarumāṟu -
yaraḻa-kkaḷai oḻinta eṭṭu meyyeḻuttukkaḷaiyum (ñ ṇ n m l v  ḷ ṉ) iṟutiyākavuṭaiya mutaṉilait toḻiṟ peyarkaḷum ēvalviṉaikaḷum, varumoḻiyāka yakaramey oḻinta piṟameykaḷai (k c t p ñ n m v) mutalākavuṭaiya coṟkaḷ varumāyiṉ, perumpāṉmaiyum ukaraccāriyai peṟṟup puṇarum. cila ēvalviṉaikaḷ avvukarac cāriyai poruntā.
ivveṭṭu meyyīṟṟu mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarkaḷum ēvalviṉaikaḷum muṟaiyē uriñ, uṇ, porun, tirum, vel, vav, tuḷ, tiṉ - eṉpaṉa. ka ca ta pa mutaliya eṭṭu meykaḷai mutalākak koṇṭa varumoḻi muṟaiyē kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, valitu, eṉpavaṟṟai mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyaroṭu puṇarkka iṭaiyē ukaraccāriyai nikaḻntu muṭiyumāṟu kāṇka.
uriñuk kaṭitu, uriñuc ciṟitu, uriñut tītu, uriñup peritu,
uriñu ñāṉṟatu, uriñu nīṇṭatu, uriñu māṇṭatu, uriñu valitu - eṉavarum.
uṇ, porun - mutaliyavaṟṟukku ukaraccāriyai koṭuttu muṭikka. vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻic cāriyaip pēṟṟiṉaiaṭuttu valleḻuttu mikutalum, piṟakaṇam varuvaḻi ukarap pēṟ ṟōṭu iyalpāka muṭitalum koḷka.
iṉi, ivveṭṭu ēvalviṉaikaḷum nilaimoḻiyāka niṟpa, ka ca ta pa mutaliya eṭṭumeykaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya varu moḻikaḷ muṟaiyē koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā, ñeḷḷā, nākā, māṭā, vaḷavā - eṉak koṇṭu cāriyaip pēṟṟiṉait  tantu muṭikka. vaṉkaṇam varumiṭattu valimiku tal illai.
	varumāṟu:	uriñu koṟṟā, uriñu cāttā, uriñu tēvā, uriñu pūtā; uriñu ñeḷḷā, uriñu nākā, uriñu māṭā, uriñu vaḷavā.
ivvāṟē uṇ, porun - mutaliyavaṟṟukku ukaraccāriyai koṭuttu muṭikka.
ukaraccāriyai peṟṟum peṟāmalum varum ēvalviṉaikaḷ ṇ ṉ l ḷ eṉṉum nāṉkumey īṟṟaṉavām. ēṉaiya nāṉku īṟukaḷum ukaram peṟṟē varum e-ṭu: uṇ koṟṟā, tiṉ cāttā, vel pūtā, tuḷ vaḷavā - ivai ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa.	(naṉ. 207)
‘muppāṟ puḷḷi’ (1) -
mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟāya āytam. (to. e.2. iḷa., nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukaram āytam - eṉṟa puḷḷi peṟum eḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum.	(cū.vi.pak. 26; to.po. (cū. 665 : 6) pak. 735 pērā.)(e.ā.pak. 13; e.ku. pak. 10,11)
“āytam eṉṟu ōcaitāṉ aṭuppukkūṭṭup pōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu” eṉpatu uṇarttutaṟku ‘āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum’ eṉṟār. itaṟku vaṭivu  kūṟiṉār,  ēṉai oṟṟukkaḷ pōla uyirēṟātu ōcaivikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟu ātaliṉ. itaṉaip puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu eṉavē, ēṉaiya eḻuttukkaḷ ellām varivaṭiviṉa ātal peṟappaṭum. (to.e. 2 nac. urai)
muppāṟ puḷḷi (2) -
āytam eṉṉum muppāṟ puḷḷi. ‘puḷḷi’ īṇṭu oliyaik kuṟikkum. ivveḻuttu nā-aṇṇam-mutalāya uṟuppuṟṟu amaiyap piṟavāmal, vāyitaḻ aṅkāppa miṭaṟṟicaiyāṉ araimāttirai yaḷavoṭu piṟappatu. ātaliṉ atu tāṉ cārntuvarum a i u eṉṉum kuṟṟuyirōcaikaḷiṉ cāyalaip peṟṟolikkum. (ekara okaraṅkaḷ ikara ukara oliyuḷ aṭaṅkum.) āytam uyir ēṟaliṉṟi yāṇṭum olippōṭu varum paṇpiṉatu eṉpatu viḷaṅka, olikkuṟip puṭaiyatākiya itaṉai vitantu ‘āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum’ eṉṟār āciriyar. ‘muppāṟpuḷḷi’ eṉavē, ataṉ varivaṭivum mukkōṇamāka amaittuk koḷḷappaṭum. āytam yāṇṭum olippoṭu niṟṟaliṉ, itaṉai uyir ūrvatillai. arai māttirai yaḷaviṟṟu ātaliṉ itu meyyiṉai ūrvatillai. iṅṅaṉam uyirukkum oṟṟukkum iṭaippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ, uyir pōlavum oṟṟup pōlavum āytam muṟaiyē alaku peṟṟum peṟātum varum.			(to. e. 2 ca. pāla.)
muraṇ eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarp puṇarcci -
muraṇ eṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar ēṉaiya toḻiṟpeyar pōla ukarac cāriyaiyum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇamāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum peṟātu, potuvitippaṭi alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum, vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilaipeṟṟup puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	: 	muraṇkaṭitu, ñekiḻntatu, yātu, iḻintatu - alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpu. muraṭkaṭumai, muraṇ ñekiḻcci, muraṇvalimai, muraṇaṭaivu - eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ṇakaram ṭakara mākat tirintum, ēṉaiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpāka vum puṇarntatu.
			muraṇ + nīṭci = muraṇīṭci - eṉa varumoḻi mutal					nakaram tirintavaḻi nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu ṇakaram 						keṭṭatu.	(to. e. 150 nac.)
			muraṇ + kaṭumai = muraṇkaṭumai, muraṭkaṭumai. 					araṇ+ kaṭumai = araṇkaṭumai, araṭkaṭumai -
eṉṟa uṟaḻcci muṭivum vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ uṇṭu.	(to.e. 309 nac.urai)
muṟṟāytam -
taṉakkuriya araimāttiraiyaik kuṟaiyāmal peṟum āytam muṟṟāytamām. atu taṉakku muṉ kuṟṟeḻuttaik koṇṭu, taṉakkup piṉṉarp perumpāṉmaiyum ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu - eṉṟa āṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu peṟṟu moḻiyiṉ iṭaiyil nikaḻum.
aḵku, kaḵcu, oṉpaḵtu - mutalāka īṟṟu valliṉa vakaiyāl varum muṟṟāytam āṟām. (kuṟṟiyalukaramē aṉṟi valliṉap puḷḷiyai ūrntu piṟa uyir variṉum aḵtu āytattai aṭuttuvarum uyirmeyyām.  e-ṭu: paḵṟi)
av+ kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya; a +kāṉ = aḵkāṉ - eṉap puṇarcci vakaiyāṉ varum muṟṟāytam oṉṟām.
ilaku - ilaḵḵku, vilaki - vilaḵḵki - eṉac ceyyuḷ vikārat tāl (virittal) varum muṟṟāytam oṉṟām.
ivvāṟṟāl muṟṟāytam eṭṭu ātal aṟiyappaṭum.			(naṉ. 90 urai)

muṟṟiyalukaram keṭṭu muṭital -
atu itu utu - eṉpaṉa aṉcāriyaiyoṭu poruntumvaḻi, ukaram keṭa, ataṉ itaṉ utaṉ - eṉavarum. avai iṉcāriyaiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ukaram keṭa, atiṉ itiṉ utiṉ - eṉa varum.	(to. e. 176 nac. urai)
āṟu eṉpatu aṟu eṉa niṉṟavaḻi, varumoḻiyāka ‘āyiram’ variṉ, aṟu  eṉpataṉ ukaram keṭa, aṟ + āyiram = aṟāyiram - eṉa muṭiyum.	(469)
cuṭṭumutalukaramē aṉṟip piṟa ukaramum uyir varuvaḻik keṭutalumuṇṭu. katavaḻakiyatu, kaḷavaḻakiyatu, kaṉavaḻakiyatu- eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṟuti ukaram keṭa niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl varumoḻi mutal akaram ēṟi muṭintavāṟu,	(176 nac.)
ellām eṉpatu vaṟṟum urupum peṟṟu īṟṟil ummai peṟuvaḻi, ellāvaṟṟaiyum- ellāvaṟṟoṭum - eṉa varum. ‘oṭu’ eṉpataṉōṭu ‘um’ cērumiṭattu, oṭuviṉ ukaram keṭa ‘oṭum’ eṉa ummoṭu puṇarum.	(189 nac.)
atu +aṉṟu, itu+aṉṟu, utu+ aṉṟu, ataṉṟu, itaṉṟu,  utaṉṟu - eṉa ukaram keṭṭu niṉṟa takara oṟṟōṭu uyir puṇarum.	(258 nac.)
muṟṟukara īṟṟup puṇarcci -
oṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟaṉurupu, āṟaṉurupu, ēḻu eṇṇum iyalpu eṇṇup peyarum oru iru - eṉṟāṟpōlat tirinta eṇṇup peyarum, muṟṟukara īṟṟu viṉaippakuti (aṭu, peṟu- pōlvaṉa), atu itu utu eṉṟa cuṭṭuppeyar - ākiyavaṟṟiṉ īṟṟu muṟṟukaram vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	cāttaṉoṭu ceṉṟāṉ - mūṉṟaṉurupu
			eṉatu talai - āṟaṉurupu
			ēḻu kaṭal - iyalpu eṇṇuppeyar
			orukal, irucol - tirinta eṇṇuppeyar
			aṭukaḷiṟu, peṟuporuḷ - muṟṟukara īṟṟu viṉaip 					pakuti
			atu ceṉṟatu, itu kaṇṭāṉ - orumaic cuṭṭup 					peyar.
ivai iyalpākap puṇarntaṉa.
utukāṇ, utukkāṇ - eṉa iyalpāyum vikāramāyum varuvaṉa arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ‘utukkāṇ’ eṉpatu uṅkē eṉṟa poruḷilvarum oṭṭi niṉṟa iṭaiccol eṉpārumuḷar. ‘uvakkāṇ’ eṉpatum uṅkē eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum oṭṭi niṉṟa iṭaiccol  (kuṟaḷ 1185 pari.)	(naṉ. 179)
muṟṟukaram keṭutal -
nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil niṟkum muṟṟiyalukaram varumoḻi mutaṟ kaṇ uyir varuvaḻik keṭutalum uṇṭu. alvaḻi vēṟṟumai - eṉa iruvaḻiyum koḷka.
e-ṭu: uyarvu + iṉitu= uyarviṉitu; katavu + aṭaittāṉ = katavaṭaittāṉ	(naṉ. 164)
muṉ: puṇarumāṟu -
muṉ eṉṟa col peyarākavum ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭap poruḷ uṇara niṉṟa iṭaiccollākavum, kuṟippu viṉai yecca īṟākavum varum. accol vaṉkaṇam variṉ iyal pākavum īṟṟu ṉakaramtirintum, ēṉaiya kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ iyalpākavum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu	:	muṉ koṇṭāṉ, muṟ koṇṭāṉ; muṉ ñāṉ ṟāṉ, muṉ vantāṉ. vaṉkaṇamvariṉ tiritalē perumpāṉmai. uyirvariṉ, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟākiya nilaimoḻi ṉakaram iraṭṭum. muṉ + aṭaintāṉ= muṉṉaṭaintāṉ.	(to. e. 333 nac.)
muṉṟil : collamaippu -
muṉ eṉṟa nilaimoḻiyai aṭuttu il eṉṟa varumoḻi puṇarumiṭattu, iṭaiyil ṉakaram taṉikkuṟil muṉ oṟṟāka iraṭṭi muṉ+ṉ+il=muṉṉil eṉa varutalē muṟai. ataṉaiviṭuttu nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu ṉakaraoṟṟai aṭuttu ataṉ iṉamākiya ṟakaraoṟṟu vantu cēra, muṉ+ṟ+il =  muṉṟil eṉa varutal ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūumuṭi pām. illattiṉatu muṉṉiṭam (muṟṟam) eṉṟu poruḷ paṭum iccol, il+muṉ= iṉmuṉ- eṉa muṟaiyē puṇarntu vārāmal muṉpiṉ nilaimāṟi muṉṟil eṉṟu vantamai marūuviṉpāṟpaṭum. iṅṅaṉam muṉpiṉ tokkaṉa vaṟṟaip piṟkālattār ilakkaṇap pōli eṉpar.			(to.e. 355 nac. urai)
itu kaṭaikkaṇ, eṉṟāṟpōla varum marūumuṭipu pōlaṉṟi, muṉṉil eṉa oṟṟu iraṭṭi muṭiyaṟpālatu, iru moḻikkum iyalpu ilatōr oṟṟu mikku muṭinta marūumuṭipu. (ṉakarattōṭu iyaiyuṭaiyatu ṟ.)	(356 iḷa. urai)
‘muṉṉatiṉ ēṉaiya muraṇutal’ -
oṉpaḵtu - nilaimoḻi;  pattu, nūṟu - varumoḻi
nilaimoḻiyākiya eṭṭiṉmēl oṉṟu, oṉpaḵtu  - eṉap paḵtu eṉṉum iṟutiyāyt tirintu niṉṟāṟ pōla, varu moḻiyākiya (eṇpatil mēl) pattai ‘nūṟu’ eṉavum, (eṇṇūṟṟiṉ mēl) nūṟṟai ‘āyiram’ eṉavum tirittu- eṉpatu poruḷ. (muṉṉatiṉ - muṉmoḻi pōla;  iṉ: aintaṉurupu)
oṉpaḵtiṉālē pattaiyum nuṟṟaiyum perukki eṉap poruḷ koḷvārumuḷar. ‘muraṇi’ eṉṟa coṟku atu poruḷaṉṟu ātalāṉum, atu poruḷāmēṉum ataṉāl varumoḻi ivvāṟu tirintatu eṉat tōṉṟāmaiyāṉum, tōṉṟiṉ nilaimoḻikkum vikāram kūṟa vēṇṭāmai yāṉum atu poruntātu eṉka.	(naṉ. 194 caṅkara.)
‘muṉṉap poruḷa’ ātal -
iraṇṭu vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷai uṇarttum coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷ, coṟkaḷ puṇarumiṭattu orēvakaiyāṉa eḻuttukkaḷāṉ ākiya toṭarkaḷāy varivaṭivil eḻutap paṭukaiyil, ittoṭarukku ipporuḷ eṉṟu collu vāṉ kuṟippāṉē amaiyum nilaiyil, avai ‘muṉṉap poruḷa’ eṉappaṭum. aḵtāvatu veḷippaṭaiyākat tiripu aṟap poruḷuṇartta iyalātaṉavāyc colluvāṉ kuṟippāṉum attoṭarai eṭuttal paṭuttal ōcaik kuṟip pāṉumē uṇarappaṭuvaṉa eṉpatu.
cempoṉ + patiṉtoṭi, cempu + oṉpatiṉ toṭi	iraṇṭum ‘cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi’ eṉṟē amaivaṉa.
cempu eṉṟa collai eṭuttu olippataṉāṉum, cem poṉ eṉpataṉai eṭuttu olippataṉāṉumē itu poruḷ teriya uṇarappaṭum. mēlum kūṟupavaṉ pēcuvatu cempaip paṟṟiyā, poṉṉaip paṟṟiyā - eṉpataṉai uṇarntālum teḷivākap poruḷ pulappaṭum.	(to. e. 142 nac. urai)
muṉṉapporuḷa āvaṉa - kuṟippiṉāṉ poruḷ uṇarap paṭum toṭarkaḷ.
muṉṉilai moḻikkaṇ iṟutiyil vārā eḻuttukkaḷ -
oḷa eṉṟa uyir, ñ n m v - eṉṟa meykaḷ, kuṟṟiyalukaram- eṉpaṉa muṉṉilai ēvalorumai viṉaikkaṇ tāmē īṟāka vārā. eṉavē, oḷavum, ñn m v - eṉṉum meykaḷum ukaram peṟṟu muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ īṟāka varum, kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟiyalukaramāka nīṇṭu muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ īṟāka varum.
	e-ṭu:	kauvu koṟṟā, kauvuk koṟṟā; uriñu koṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā; porunu koṟṟā, porunuk koṟṟā; tirumu koṟṟā, tirumuk koṟṟā; tevvu koṟṟā, tevvukkoṟṟā; kūṭṭu koṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā - ivvīṟukaḷ āṟum ukaram peṟṟu valliṉam mikāmalum mikkum uṟaḻntaṉa.	(to. e. 152 nac.)
muṉṉilai moḻikku oḷa ñ n m v kuṟṟiyalukaram ivaṟṟai vilakkiyataṉ kāraṇam -
muṉṉilai orumai vikutikaḷ i ai āy - eṉpaṉa. ikaram nī eṉṟa peyariluḷḷa īkārattiṉ tiripu. āy eṉpatu ai eṉpataṉ nīṇṭa vaṭivē. aiyaṉ eṉṟa peyariṉ mutaṉilai ai. oḷavai eṉṟa peyariṉ mutaṉilai oḷa. kaṉṉaṭa moḻi yil āṭūu muṉṉilaiviṉaikaḷil ai-cērntu ‘kēḷvai’ eṉa varum; makaṭūu muṉṉilaiviṉaikaḷil oḷa- cērntu ‘kēḷ veḷa’ eṉa varum. ivvāṟē tamiḻilum paṇṭaik kālattil āṭūu muṉṉilaiviṉaikkaṇ aiyum, makaṭūu muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ oḷavum cērttu vaḻaṅkiṉar. piṟkālattup paṭarkkaikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi muṉṉilaik kaṇṇum aḵṟiṇai vaḻakku vantamaiyāl avvēṟupāṭu vēṇṭā eṉa oḻikkappaṭṭapōtu, oḷa muṉṉilaikkaṇ varutal nīkkappaṭṭatu.
muṉṉilaip paṉmaikkaṇ ceyyum- irum- eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ēvalil kāṇum umvikuti paṇṭaic ceyyuḷil kāṇappaṭa villai. ‘uṇm eṉa irakkum’ (puṟanā. 178) ‘tiṉm eṉat tarutaliṉ’ (150) - eṉa makara īṟṟu muṉṉilaic col vantuḷḷaṉa. iccoṟkaḷai nōkka, makaramey paṉmaiyaik kuṟikkiṟatu. ceykum- varutum- eṉṟa taṉmaippaṉmai muṟṟup pōla, uṇm- tiṉm- eṉpaṉa muṉṉilaip paṉmai viṉaiyaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. centamiḻil nīm eṉpataṉkaṇ uḷḷa makarattai vilakkiyataṉāl, viṉaikkaṇṇum uṇm eṉpatu pōla makara īṟu vārātu vilakkappaṭṭatu. oḷavum makaramum tol. kālattu muṉṉarē vilakkappaṭṭaṉa.
moḻiyiṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum muṟṟiyalukaramāka olikkum. avvāṟu olittal muṉṉilaiviṉaikkaṇṇē eṉpatum, āṇṭup poruḷ vēṟupaṭum eṉpatum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu - eṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṟkum eṉṟu itaṉai viḷakkiyuḷḷār eṉpataṉai nōkkak kuṟṟiya lukarattu iṟuti muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ vārātu eṉpatum peṟappaṭum.
ñakaram ‘uriñ’ eṉat toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṉkaṇṇum, nakaram ‘verin’ eṉṟa peyarccoṟkaṇṇum ‘porun’ eṉṟa toḻiṟpeyar oṉṟaṉkaṇṇum varumātaliṉ  avai viṉaikkaṇ vārā. “ñ n- muṉṉilai ēvaṟkaṇ varum; āṇṭu avai ukaram peṟum” eṉṟu naṉṉūlār kūṟiyatu pōlat tol. kuṟippiṭāmaiyāl, ña na-kkaḷ toḻiṟ peyarkkaṇ aṉṟi muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ vārā.
vakaram av iv uv - eṉṉum cuṭṭukkaḷilum, tev eṉṉum peyarccollilumaṉṟi varutal iṉmaiyiṉ, atu muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ vārātu eṉa vilakkappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu oḷa- ñ n m v -  kuṟṟiyalukaram - eṉpaṉa muṉṉilai viṉaikkaṇ vārā eṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉa.	(e. ā. pak. 115, 116)
muṉṉilai moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟātaṉa -
muṉṉilai moḻiyāvatu muṉṉilaiviṉai. muṉniṉṟāṉ toḻil uṇarttuvaṉavum muṉniṉṟāṉait toḻiṟ paṭuttuvaṉavum - eṉa muṉṉilaiviṉai iru vakaittu. muṉniṉ ṟāṉait toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavaṟṟait teḷivu karuti ‘ēval viṉai’ eṉṟu kūṟalām. ivvēval viṉai orumaikku ellā īṟṟu viṉaippakutikaḷum payaṉpaṭalām;eṉṟālum oḷa eṉṉum uyirīṟu, ñ n m v - eṉṉum puḷḷiyīṟu, kuṟṟiya lukara īṟu - eṉpaṉa muṉṉilai ēval orumai viṉaiyāka vārā. ivai ēval viṉaiyāka varavēṇṭumāyiṉ, uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum ukaram peṟṟu varavēṇṭum; kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu muṟṟiyalukara īṟākiviṭum.
	e-ṭu:	kauvu koṟṟā; uriñu koṟṟā, porunu koṟṟā, tirumu koṟṟā, tevvu koṟṟā, kūṭṭu koṟṟā - ivaṟṟuḷ varumoḻi valleḻuttu vikaṟpittu mikutal koḷka.
kūṭṭu eṉpatu ēvalorumai muṟṟākiyavaḻi muṟṟiyalukara īṟṟatu.	(to.e.152 nac. urai)
kuṟṟiyalukaraīṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikkuk kūṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum (iyalpātalē aṉṟi). (153 iḷa.urai)
muṉṉilai viṉai, ēval -
muṉṉilaiviṉai eṉpatu taṉmaiviṉai paṭarkkaiviṉai kaṭku iṉamākiya muṉṉilai viṉaiyaik kuṟikkum. iṉamiṉṟi muṉṉilai yoṉṟaṟkē uriyatu ēvalviṉai. ākaliṉ muṉ ṉilaiviṉai eṉpatu ēvalviṉaiyai uṇarttāmaiyiṉ ‘ēval’ eṉat taṉiyē ataṉai vitantu kūṟiṉār.			(naṉ. 161 caṅkara.)
mūvakaik kuṟaikaḷ -
aṭitoṭai mutaliya nōkkit tokukkumvaḻit tokuttal aṉṟi, vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marūuppōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ marūuvāy, aṭippāṭāka orumoḻi mutal iṭai kaṭai eṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum kuṟaintu varutalum ceyyuḷvikāramām.			(naṉ. 156 civañā.)
mūvakaikkuṟai ceyyuḷmarūu aṉmai -
mutal iṭai kaṭaik kuṟaiyākiya mūṉṟum vaḻakkiṉkaṇ marūup pōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ marūuvāy aṭippāṭāka varum eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvar. atu poruntātu, aṟuvakai vikāraṅkaḷum ceyyuḷ ceyyum cāṉṟōr aḻaku peṟa acceyyuḷil vēṇṭuḻi varuvikka varum eṉpataṉṟē? atai nōkki iccūttirattai māṭṭeṟintār ātalāl, itaṟkum ceyyuṭ ceyyum cāṉṟōr varuvittuḻi varum eṉpatē karuttu. itu karutiyaṉṟē ‘ovvoru moḻi’ eṉpatai ‘orumoḻi’ eṉa ivar iccūttiram ceytār? iṉṉum atu poruntātu eṉpataṟkup ‘pacumpuṟ ṟalaikāṇ paritu’ (kuṟaḷ 16) eṉpatil kāṇpatu eṉpatu ‘kāṇpu’ eṉak kaṭaik kuṟaintatum, ‘cāṉṟōr eṉpilar tōḻi’ eṉpatil ‘cāṉṟōr eṉpār ilar’ eṉpatu ‘eṉpilar’ eṉak kaṭaik kuṟaintatumē cāṉṟātal uṇarka. iṉi, vaḻakkiṭattum ilaikkaṟiyai ‘laikkaṟi’ eṉavum, ‘nilā utittatu’ eṉpataṟku ‘lā utittatu’ eṉavum, ‘irāppakal’ eṉpataṟku ‘rāppakal’ eṉavum, kēḻvaraku ‘kēvaraku’, nīrccilai ‘nīccīlai’, pōki ṟāṉ ‘pōṟāṉ’, pāṭukiṟāṉ ‘pāṭuṟāṉ’ eṉavum, ‘āṉai yēṟum perumpaṟaiyaṉ’ eṉṉum marapu paṟṟi vanta ‘tōṭṭi yāṉ eṉpatait ‘tōṭṭi’ eṉavum taṇṇīr ‘taṇṇī’ vennīr ‘vennī’ eṉavum muṟaiyē immūvakai vikāraṅkaḷum varutal koḷka. 	(naṉ. 156 irāmā.)
mūvakaip peyareccak kuṟippu muṉ vali variṉ iyalpātal -
amarmukatta, kaṭuṅkaṇṇa, ciṟiya, periya, uḷa, ila, pala, cila (ivai peyaraṭiyākap piṟanta peyareccakkuṟippu): poṉṉaṉṉa (iḵtu iṭaiccollaṭiyākap piṟantatu): kaṭiya (iḵtu uriccollaṭiyākap piṟantatu) - ivaṟṟai nilaimoḻiyākavum, kutirai - cennāy -  takar- paṉṟi - eṉpa vaṟṟai varumoḻiyākavum koṇṭu puṇarppavē, iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 167 caṅkara.)
mūvakai meyt tōṟṟattukku uriya vaḷikaḷ -
valliṉam talaivaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu; melliṉam mūkku vaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu; iṭaiyiṉam miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ piṟappatu. (to.e. 88 nac. urai)
mūvarāvāṉ oru karuttaṉ -
cey eṉṉum ēvalviṉaiyiṉ piṉpu vi-pi- eṉṉum iraṇ ṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu variṉ, ceyvi eṉṉum poruḷaip peṟum. ivaiyiraṇṭum oruṅku variṉum, oṉṟē iṇaintu variṉum ēvalmēl ēval tōṉṟa, mūvarāvāṉ oru karuttaṉaik kāṭṭum. (ēvuvār mūvar; iyaṟṟutal karuttā oruvaṉ.)
	e-ṭu :	naṭappi, varuvi, maṭivi, cīppi, kēṭpi, aḵkuvi-
			ivai ceyvi eṉṉum ēvalviṉaip pakāppatam.
			naṭattuvippi, varuvippi, naṭappippi, kaṟpippi - vipi eṉpaṉa iraṇṭum iṇaintum oṉṟē iṇain tum vanta ceyvippi eṉṉum ēvalmēl ēval pakāppatam.	(naṉ. 137 mayilai.)
mūvaḷapu icaittal -
mūṉṟu māttirai aḷavu olittal. itu taṉitta ōreḻut tiṟku iṉṟu. eṉavē, ībḻuttuk kūṭiya iṭattēyē mūṉṟu māttirai peṟum eṉpatu. itaṉāl aḷapeṭai eṉpatu neṭilai aṭutta iṉakkuṟilē eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. vaṭa moḻiyil ōreḻuttē aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl mūṉṟu māttirai aḷavu olikkum eṉpatu tamiḻ nūlārkku uṭaṉpāṭiṉmai peṟappaṭum.	(to. e. 5)
mūviṭattum neṭilēḻum aḷapeṭuttal -
vāakai, īikai, ūukam, pēekaṉ, taiiyal, tōokai,  mauuval - eṉavum,
paṭāakai, parīikam, kaḻuumaṇi, parēeram vaḷaiiyam, purōocai, maṉauukam - eṉavum,
kurāa, kurīi, kuḻūu, vilēe, viraii, ulōo, aṉauu - eṉavum muṟaiyē moḻi mutal iṭai kaṭai eṉṟa mūviṭat tum ēḻneṭṭeḻuttum aḷapeṭuttaṉa.	(naṉ. 90 mayilai.)
mūviṭattu makara īṟṟup peyar alvaḻip puṇarcci -
mūviṭattu makara īṟṟup peyarkaḷ ellārum, ellīrum, tām, nām, yām, ellām - eṉpaṉa. avai varumoḻi vaṉkaṇa māyiṉ īṟṟu makaram iṉamelleḻuttāyt tiriyum.
varumāṟu: ellāruṅkuṟiyar, ellīruṅkuṟiyīr, tāṅkuṟiyar, tāṅ kuṟiya, nāṅ kuṟiyam, yāṅ kuṟiyēm.
meṉkaṇamākiya ñakaramum nakaramum variṉ, ellāruñ ñāṉṟār, ellārun nīṇṭār, ellīruñ ñāṉṟīr, ellīrun nīṇṭīr, tāñ ñāṉṟār, tān nīṇṭār, nāñ ñāṉṟām, nān nīṇṭām, yāñ ñāṉṟām, yān nīṇṭām - eṉa varumoḻi melleḻuttākiya ñakara nakaramāka makaram tiriyum.
iṭaiyiṉamum uyirum variṉ, ellārum yāttār, vantār, aṭaintār - eṉa makaram iyalpākap puṇarum. ellām eṉpatu vaṉkaṇam variṉ īṟṟu makaram keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarntu iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyai peṟum. ellām+kuṟiyaḻū ellā +kuṟiyaḻū ellāk + kuṟiya+um= ellāk kuṟiyavum. ēṉaik kaṇaṅkaḷ variṉ makaraīṟu keṭṭu varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarntu iṟutikkaṇ ummuc cāriyai peṟum.
ellām+ ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, vaṭṭu, aṭai= ellā ñāṇum, ellānūlum, ellāmaṇiyum, ellāvaṭṭum, ellā vaṭaiyum (vakaram uṭampaṭumey)
ellām vāṭiṉa, ellāmāṭiṉa - eṉa iyalpāka muṭitalum koḷka.
ciṟupāṉmai ellām eṉpatu, vali variṉ makaram keṭṭu melimikku iṟutiyil ummup peṟṟu ellāṅ kuṟiyavum, ellāñ ciṟiyavum - eṉa varum.
uyartiṇaikkaṇ ellām eṉpatu, ellāk kollarum, ellā nāykarum, ellā vaṇikarum, ellā aracarum - eṉa īṟu keṭṭu, vaṉkaṇattu valimikku ēṉaiya kaṇattu iyalpākap puṇarntum,
ellāṅkuṟiyarum, ellāñciṟiyarum- eṉa īṟu keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikkup puṇarntum, ivviru tiṟattum iṟutiyil ummuccāriyai peṟṟu muṭital koḷka.
ellāṅ kuṟiyar, ellāṅ kuṟiyīr, ellāṅ kuṟiyēm - eṉa īṟu keṭṭu iṉamelleḻuttu mikku iṟutikkaṇ ummup peṟātu varutalum, ellām vantēm, ellā maṭaintēm- eṉa iṭaikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum variṉ iyalpākap puṇartalumuḷa.
num eṉpatu, ‘nīir’ eṉat tirintu payaṉilai koṇṭu muṭiyum.  e-ṭu : nīir kaṭiyir.	(to.e.320- 323 , 326 nac.)
mūviṭattum makaraīṟṟup peyar vēṟṟumaip puṇarcci -
ellārum eṉpatu tammuccāriyaiyum, ellīrum eṉpatu nummuccāriyaiyum, ellām eṉpatu aḵṟiṇaikkaṇ vaṟṟuccāriyaiyum uyartiṇaikkaṇ nammuccāriyaiyum peṟṟuvarum. ellārum ellīrum eṉpavaṟṟu iṟuti ummuc cāriyai pirittu varumoḻikkup piṉṉark kūṭṭappeṟum.
e-ṭu :	ellārtaṅkaiyum, ellīrnuṅkaiyum - eṉavum ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum, ellānaṅkaiyum - eṉavum
tam num nam - eṉpavaṟṟatu  makarattai varumoḻi valleḻut tiṟ kēṟpa iṉamelleḻuttākat tirittum, ellām eṉpataṉ īṟṟu makarattaik keṭuttum puṇarkka. iyalpu kaṇam variṉum ic cāriyaikaḷ varum.
e-ṭu :	ellārtamyāḻum, vaṭṭum, aṇiyum - eṉavum
		ellīrnumyāḻum, vaṭṭum, aṇiyum - eṉavum
		ellāvaṟṟuyāppum, valiyum, aṭaivum - eṉavum
ellānamyāppum, valiyum, aṭaivum - eṉavum varumāṟu kāṇka.
tām nām yām - eṉpaṉa tam nam em - eṉṟāki varumoḻik kēṟpa. īṟṟu makaram keṭa, iṉamelleḻuttu mikku,
taṅkai, tañcevi, tantalai - eṉa varum. (naṅkai.... eṅkai..... mutalākak koḷka.)
meṉkaṇattu makaramum iṭaiyiṉamum uyirum variṉ iyalpāyum, taṉikkuṟilmuṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭiyum muṭitalum koḷka.
	e-ṭu:	taññāṇ, tannūl - īṟṟu makaram keṭa, varumoḻi melloṟṟu iraṭṭutal.
			tammaṇi, tamyāḻ, tamvaṭṭu - iyalpu
			tam + ilai (tammilai) - taṉikkuṟil muṉ makaram uyirvara iraṭṭutal
			(nam em - eṉpavaṟṟoṭum oṭṭuka.)
tama kāṇam, ema kāṇam, numa kāṇam - eṉa īṟu akarac cāriyai peṟutalumuṇṭu.
nummiṉ makaram vali variṉ keṭṭu melloṟṟu mikkum, ñakara nakaraṅkaḷ variṉ avvoṟṟu iraṭṭiyum, makara yakara vakaraṅkaḷ variṉ  iyalpāyum, uyirvariṉ makara oṟṟu iraṭṭiyum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu :	nuñcevi; nuññāṇ, nunnūl; nummaṇi, num yāḻ, numvaṭṭu; nummāṟṟal.
ellārum ellīrum - eṉpaṉa iṭaiyē tam num - cāriyai peṟāmal ellārkaiyum ellīrkaiyum - eṉṟāṟ pōla varumoḻiyoṭu puṇartalumuṇṭu.			(to. e. 320, 322, 324, 325 nac. urai)
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaip puṇarcci: ciṟappu viti -
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai eḻuvāy nilaimoḻiyāy niṟpa, av veḻuvāyākiya nilaimoḻipporuḷāl ākiṉṟa ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiccol varumoḻiyāka varumiṭattu, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ varum valliṉam potuvitiyāl mikku muṭitalē yaṉṟi, uṟaḻcciyum iyalpum ākum. uyirīṟu meyyīṟu- eṉṟa iruvakai  nilaimoḻiyum koḷka.  ippuṇarmoḻi mūṉṟāmvēṟṟumaittokai.
	e-ṭu:	arāt tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, cuṟāp pāyappaṭṭāṉ - eṉṟu mikku muṭitalē perumpāṉmai.
	pēykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pēykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ;		uṟaḻcci
	pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ  	ṉū
	pēypiṭikkappaṭṭāṉ, pulikaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ	iyalpu.
kōṭpaṭutal eṉpatu mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai eḻuvāyāl oru cāttaṟku ēṟpaṭṭa nilai. koḷḷutal mūṉṟām vēṟṟumai eḻuvāyiṉ toḻil.
tam toḻil:	cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi, cāttaṉākiya eḻuvāyiṉ toḻil.
tammiṉākiya toḻil : cāttaṉ puli kaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ eṉpuḻi, ōr eḻuvāyāl cāttaṉukku ēṟpaṭṭa toḻil; aḵtāvatu kaṭikkappaṭutal.	(naṉ. 256)
‘mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttu’ -
mūṉṟai muṭiyilē yiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavē, muppattu mūṉṟākum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.
pēreṇṇai muṉṉark kūṟicciṟṟeṇṇai aṭuttuk kūṟutal tamiḻmarapu. ciṟṟeṇṇai muṉṉark kūṟip pēreṇṇaip piṉṉark kūṟutal vaṭamoḻi marapu.
‘mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatu’ eṉpatu kāttiyāyaṉar matam. muppattu mūṉṟu: tamiḻmarapu.
moḻikku mutalākum eṉṟa 22 eḻuttukkaḷum, moḻikku īṟākum eṉṟa 24 eḻuttukkaḷum tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷ muppattu mūṉṟil aṭaṅkuvaṉavē.	(to.e.103 nac.)
‘mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru ātal’ -
uyirmey eḻuttil uyiraip pirittāl mey taṉṉuṭaiya paḻaiya vaṭivattaip peṟum.
ālilai - āl + ilai; ataṉai - ataṉ + ai
eṉavē, uyirmey eṉpatu pirikkum nilaiyil amainta kalappeḻuttām.	(to. e. 139 nac.)
‘mey piṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟkai ātal’ -
ṇakara ṉakara īṟṟu meykaḷ nilaimoḻi īṟākumiṭattu varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ muṟaiyē ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷāy mey piṟitākum (vaṭivu tiriyum)- eṉṟa viti payaṉpaṭuttappaṭa vēṇṭiya iṭattu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaippuṇarcciyāyiṉ, attiripu peṟātu iyalpākap puṇartal.
	e-ṭu:	maṇ+ kuṭam = maṭkuṭam - mey piṟitu ātal
			poṉ +kuṭam = poṟkuṭam - mey piṟitu ātal
			maṇ + koṇarntāṉ = maṇ koṇarntāṉ
			poṉ + koṇarntāṉ = poṉ koṇarntāṉ - eṉa ṇakara. ṉakara īṟukaḷ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. (to.e. 157 nac.)
mey piṟitu ātal -
itu puṇarcciyil nikaḻum mūvakait tiripukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. mey piṟitu ātal - vaṭivu vēṟupaṭutal.
	e-ṭu	:	maṇ+ kuṭam = maṭkuṭam - ṇakaram ṭakaramāy vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭatu. col + kēṭṭāṉ= coṟ kēṭṭāṉ - lakaram ṟakaramāy vaṭivu vēṟupaṭṭatu.
			yāṉ + ai = eṉṉai- ‘yā’ eṉpatu ‘e’eṉa vaṭivu vēṟupaṭa, ṉakaram iraṭṭittatu.
iṅṅaṉam ōreḻuttup piṟitōr eḻuttāyt tiriyum tiri piṉait tol. ‘mey piṟitātal’ eṉṟār.	(to.e. 109 nac.)
‘meymmai yākal’ -
iyalpākap puṇartal. meymmai paṭṭāṅku ātaliṉ, iyalpām. nāy+ kōṭpaṭṭāṉ = nāy kōṭpaṭṭāṉ - itu ‘meymmai yātal’ eṉṉum iyalpu puṇarcci.			(to.e. 156 nac. urai)
meymayakkam -
oru colliṉkaṇṇum puṇarmoḻiyiṉkaṇṇum oru mey taṉṉoṭum piṟameykaḷoṭum kūṭum kūṭṭam. ‘iṭainilai meymayakkam’, kāṇka.
meymayakkam : uṭaṉilaimayakkam, vēṟṟunilai mayakkam -
mey patiṉeṭṭaṉuḷḷum k c t p - eṉṉum nāṉkum oḻittu niṉṟa patiṉālu meyyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟu māṟi vantu oṉṟuvatu (vēṟṟunilai) meymayakkamām. rakara ḻakaram oḻittu niṉṟa patiṉāṟu meyyum tam muṉṉart tām vantu oṉṟuvatu uṭaṉilai mayakkamām. iccollappaṭṭa iru kūṟṟu muppatu mayakkum moḻiyiṭaiyilēyām. uyirmey mayakkam iṉṉataṉ piṉṉar iṉṉatām eṉṟa varaiyaṟai illai.	(naṉ. 109 mayilai.)
meymayakkam orumoḻi irumoḻikkaṇ kōṭal -
iṉṉa meykku iṉṉa mey naṭpeḻuttu, iṉṉamey pakaiyeḻuttu eṉpataṉai uṭkoṇṭē meymayakkamum puṇarccivitikaḷum amaintaṉa.
lakaraḷakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉ yakaram mayaṅkum eṉpataṉukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭut tarumpōtu, kolyāṉai, veḷyāṟu- eṉṟa viṉaittokai paṇputtokaikaḷai oru moḻiyākak koṇṭu nac. kuṟippiṭṭu, ittokaiyallāt taṉi moḻikkaṇ coṟkaḷ āciriyarkālattu iruntu piṉṉar iṟantaṉapōlum eṉṟu karutukiṟār.
iṅṅaṉamē ñn m v - eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar yakaram vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriñ yātu, porun yātu, tirum yātu, tev yātu - eṉṟu irumoḻikaḷaip puṇarttu eṭuttukkāṭṭut tarutalai virumpātu, utāraṇaṅkaḷ iṟantaṉa eṉṟār. nūl marapu orumoḻikkuḷ amaiyum ceytikaḷaic collavē amaiyum ātaliṉ, oru collilēyē mey mayakkam varum ceytiyaik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu nac. karuttu. iru moḻikkaṇ varum meymayakkam ‘puṇarcci’ eṉṟa vēṟu peyaril amaitaliṉ, ataṉai meymayakkattuḷ aṭakkutal kūṭātu eṉpatu avar karuttu.	(to. e. 24, 27 nac. urai)
meymayakkam orumoḻikkum puṇarmoḻikkum potu vātaliṉ, mēṟkūṟiya puṇarmoḻic ceykaiyellām talaiyāya aṟiviṉōrai nōkka oruvāṟṟāṉ kūṟiyavā ṟāyiṟṟu eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.	(23 iḷa. urai) 

meymayakkam nikaḻum iṭaṉ -
tol. taṉimoḻi ilakkaṇattai moḻimarapiṉkaṇ kūṟiyuḷḷār; meymayakkattai nūlmarapil kūṟiyuḷḷār. immeymayakkam oru moḻikkaṇ koḷḷa vēṇṭuvatōr ilakkaṇamāyiṉ, āciriyar taṉimoḻiyiṉaip paṟṟik kūṟum moḻimarapil kūṟiyiruppār. moḻiyiṉ mutalil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ, īṟṟil niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷ ivaṟṟaik kūṟumiṭattēyē moḻiyiṭai niṟkum meymayakkattaik kūṟa vēṇṭum. moḻimarapil kūṟātu nulmarapil meymayakkam kūṟiya ataṉāl, itaṉai orumoḻikkaṇṇēyē koḷḷa vēṇṭuvataṉṟu. iṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭa ceyti, iṉṉa mey yeḻuttiṉōṭu iṉṉa meyyeḻuttu mayaṅkum eṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉaik kūṟiya attuṇaiyē ākum. itaṉai iṭainilai mayakkam eṉṟu kūṟuvatu takkataṉṟu; meymayak kam eṉṟalē takum. immayakkattiṟkum puṇarnilaikkum vēṟṟumai uṇṭu. puṉkāl, palkutal - mutaliya coṟkaḷil kakarattoṭu mayaṅkiya ṉakara lakara meykaḷ, cantiyil poṟ kuṭam, pāṟkuṭam- eṉṟāṟ pōlat tirivaṉa āyiṉa.			(e. ā. pak. 23, 24)
meymayakkam nulmarapil kūṟappaṭṭa kāraṇam -
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷum īṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷum moḻiyiṭaippaṭuttu uṇarappaṭa vēṇṭu taliṉ, taṉiyeḻuttup paṟṟiya nūlmarapil kūṟappaṭātu, moḻiyiṭaippaṭuttu uṇarappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum moḻimarapil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. moḻiyiṭaiyē varum iṭainilai meymayakkamum moḻiyiṭaik kāṇappaṭuva toṉṟātaliṉ atuvum moḻimarapil vaikkappaṭa vēṇṭu meṉiṉ, moḻikku iṭaiyē varum eḻutteṉṉātu, iv veḻuttukku ivveḻuttu naṭpu, allaṉa pakai - eṉṟa ceytiyaiyē viḷakkutaliṉ, icceyti nulmarapil kūṟap paṭṭatu.	(cū.vi. pak. 57)
‘meyyiṉ  iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇi’yē varutal -
taṉimey araimāttirai aḷaviṟṟu. atu nāc ciṟitu puṭai peyarum ciṟṟoli ātaliṉ, taṉimeyyaik kūṟik kāṭṭa lākātu. ākavē, akara uyiroṭu cērttu meyyiṉai uyirmeyyāka olittalum, akaram ēṟiya meyyākak kūṟik kāṭṭalum marapāyiṉa.
	e-ṭu :
‘valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa’	(to.e. 19 nac.)
‘melleḻut teṉpa ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa’	(to.e. 20 nac.)
‘iṭaiyeḻut teṉpa yarala vaḻaḷa’	(to. e. 21 nac.)
ivvāṟu meykaḷ akarattoṭu civaṇiyē olivaṭivilum varivaṭivilum kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.	(to. e. 46 nac.)
meyyiṉ iyaṟkai -
uruvāki vaṭivu peṟum meyyeḻuttukkaḷiṉ taṉmai yāvatu, avai olippu uṭaiyaṉavāyum niṟṟal. puḷḷi yoṭum+ nilaiyal = puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal. ummaiyāṉ, puḷḷiyoṭu nillāmal uyirōṭiyaintu uyirmeyyākavum niṟkum eṉakkoḷka. (uyir mey - uyirkkum mey) meyyāṉatu olippiṉṟi uruvātalum, uyirppu unta arai māttiraiyaḷavu olittalum, uyir iyaiya icaittalum ākiya mūṉṟu nilaikaḷai yuṭaittu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.			(to. e. 15 ca. pāla.)
meyyum oṟṟum puḷḷiyum -
patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷum vaṭivu koḷḷum nilaimaikkaṇ cevip pulaṉāvatillaiātaliṉ, annilai mey eṉṟum oṟṟu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. avaṟṟaic cevippulaṉākka vēṇṭiṉ, akaram mutaliya uyirppicaikaḷāṉ untāmal, aṉukaraṇa ōcaiyai uyirppākki unta avai cevippula ṉākum. aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttat tuṇaipuriyum ōcaiyai araimāttiraiyaḷavē icaittal vēṇṭum eṉa, ‘meyyiṉ aḷavē araiyeṉa moḻipa’ eṉpar tolkāppiyaṉār. ammeyyiṉatu muḻuttaṉmaiyum pulappaṭutta vēṇṭiṉ, akaravuyiraic cāriyaiyākak koṭuttu ataṉāl veḷip paṭuttal vēṇṭum. itaṉai āciriyar ‘meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum’ eṉṟār.
ivvāṟāka aṉukaraṇa uyirppicaiyum akaraccāriyaiyum iṉṟi ammeykaḷ moḻiyuṟuppāka ōr uyireḻuttaiyō uyirmey yeḻuttaiyō cārntu avaṟṟiṉpiṉ varumiṭattu naṉku olikkum annilaiyai (al-kāl-aval- kāval) āciriyar ‘puḷḷi’ eṉac cuṭṭik kūṟuvar. ākaliṉ puḷḷimey orumoḻiyiṉ iṭaiyilum īṟṟilumē niṟkum. itaṉai ‘meyyīṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal’ eṉṟār.
olippuṟṟu niṟkum puḷḷimeykaḷ annilaimaikkaṇ uyir ēṟa iṭam tārā; olippunilai nīṅki vaṭivukoṇṭa muṉṉaiya nilaiyiltāṉ uyirēṟa iṭam tarum. itaṉaip ‘puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu, meyyoṭu civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē’ eṉpār āciriyar. aṅṅaṉam meyyiṉai ūrntu ammeyyiṉai veḷippaṭuttuṅkāl, uyir taṉ talaimaippāṭṭiṉaiyum piṟappiṭattaiyum iḻantu meykkut tuṇaiyākiṟatu. mey talaimaip pāṭuṟṟu uyir mey eṉa nikaḻkiṟatu. ātaliṉ uyirmeyyiṉai meyyiṉ piṟitoru nilaiyākavē āciriyar kūṟukiṟār. ‘puḷḷi yillā ellā meyyum... uyirtta lāṟē’ eṉpatu tol. cūttiram.			(to. e. pak. 11 ca. pāla.)


meyyeḻuttiṉ ilakkaṇam -
meyyeḻuttut taṉittolikkum iyalpiṉṟi, vāyuṟuppuk kaḷāṉ uruvāki vaṭivuṟṟu aṉukaraṇa ōcaiyāṉ olinilai eyti, taṭaiyuṟṟu veṭittum aṭaivuṟṟu naḻuviyum taṭai yiṉṟi uraciyum nekiḻntum, neñcuvaḷi - miṭaṟṟuvaḷi - mūkkuvaḷi - icaikaḷāṉ veḷippaṭṭuc cevippulaṉām; uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉpiṉ uyiricai alakiṉ tuṇaiyāṉ puḷḷiyuṟṟu olikkum; vāyuṟuppuk kaḷiṉ toḻil muyaṟciyum vaḷiyicaiyum kāraṇamāka vaṉmai - meṉmai - iṭaimai - eṉṉum taṉmaikaḷaip peṟum; uyiricaiyait tuṇaiyākak koṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ untu talāṉ uyirmeyyāki icaittu alaku peṟum; uyirum kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum ēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu, av vaḻit taṉatu māttiraiyai iḻantu, ēṟiya uyiriṉ māttirai yaḷavē taṉakku aḷavākak koṇṭu, uyirmey eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu peṟṟu niṟkum; moḻikkaṇ viṭṭicai yātu toṭarntu varum;uṭaṉniṟṟaṟku ēlāta mey iṇaiya varuvaḻit tirintu piḷavupaṭāmal toṭarum;  taṉakkup piṉ varum uyirmeyyiṉ oliyai mayakkit tiripupaṭuttum; uyiraik kūṭiyō cārntō collāyum colluṟuppā yum amaintu poruḷ viḷakkum; paṇṇicaikaḷaiyum vaṇṇaṅkaḷaiyum uṇṭākkum; uyirppicaikaḷai ēṟṟu neṭumai - kuṟumai - eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭu peṟum; tammai ūrnta uyirkaḷukku vaṉmai - meṉmai - iṭaimai - eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭukaḷai eytuvikkum;tām uyirmey āvataṟkut tuṇaiceyyum uyirkaḷait tamakkuriya piṟappiṭattiṉiṉṟu piṟakkac ceytu tāmē talaimai peṟum;mey-puḷḷi- uyirmey- eṉṉum mūvakaiyākac ceyaṟpāṭu peṟṟuvarum.		(to. e. pak. 19, 20 ca. pāla.)
‘meyyoṭu civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē’ -
niṟutta colliṉ iṟuti niṟkum puḷḷiyeḻuttiṉ muṉ kuṟittuvarucolliṉ mutal varum uyirtāṉ piḷavupaṭṭu icaikkāmal, arai māttirai olippuṭaiyatāy niṟkum puḷḷiyeḻuttiṉ iyalpiṉaik keṭuttu, olippu illāta meyvaṭivākki ataṉoṭu porunti icaikkum.
	e-ṭu	:	nūl +aḻaku =nūlaḻaku; 	pāl + āṟu =pālāṟu;	āl+ilai =ālilai; aruḷ +īkai = aruḷīkai;	pukaḻ + oḷaviyam =pukaḻeḷaviyam				(to. e.  138 ca. pāla.)
mey: vēṟu peyarkaḷ -
ūmai eṉiṉum, oṟṟu eṉiṉum, uṭal eṉiṉum, kāttiram eṉiṉum meyyeṉṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī. eḻut. 13)
melliṉam -
ṅñṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa āṟu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum mel leṉṟa mūkkoliyāl piṟappaṉa ātaliṉ mel liṉattaic cārntaṉavām. (im melleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭam mūkku eṉṉum naṉṉūl. muttu vīriyam eḻut. 43ām nuṟpā mel liṉam āṟum piṟakkum iṭam talai eṉkiṟatu.)	(naṉ. 69)
melleḻuttu āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya peyarātal -
ṅ ñ ṇ n m ṉ - eṉṟa āṟu meykaḷum melleḻuttām. ivaṟṟaip piṉṉar ‘melleḻuttu mikiṉum’(323), ‘mel leḻuttu iyaṟkai’- (145) eṉṟāṟpōla eṭuttuk kuṟippiṭa vēṇṭiyum, ivai meṉmaiyāṉa mūkkoliyāṉ piṟattal karutiyum ivaṟṟai melleḻuttu eṉṟār.			(to.e. 20 nac. urai)
melleḻuttu iyaṟkai uṟaḻtal -
moḻikku mutalil varuvaṉa valliṉa uyirmey - 4, melliṉa uyirmey -3, iṭaiyiṉa uyirmey-2, uyir - 12 eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ valliṉam allāta meṉmai iṭaimai uyirkkaṇaṅkaḷ ‘iyalpu kaṇam’ eṉappaṭum. ivai varu moḻi mutalil variṉ, nilaimoḻi īṟṟilō varumoḻi mutalilō tiripu ēṟpaṭāmal iyalpākap puṇartalē perumpāṉmai. āṉāl mutalmoḻi toṭarmoḻiyāy niṟka ataṉai aṭuttu varum meṉkaṇam  iyalpātalē yaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmai mikkum puṇarum.
	e-ṭu : 	katir+ñeri = katirñeri, katirññeri; katir +nuṉi = katirnuṉi, katirnnuṉi; katir +muri = katirmuri, katirmmuri
ivvāṟu melleḻuttu iyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭitalu muṇṭu.	(to. e. 145 nac.)
melleḻuttu mikā marappeyarkaḷ -
‘peṟunavum’ eṉṟa ummaiyāl, attikkāy - akattikkāy - āttikkāy - iṟalikkāy- ilantaikkāy - mutalāyiṉa melleḻuttup peṟā eṉak koḷka.	(naṉ.165 mayilai.)
melleḻuttu: vēṟu peyarkaḷ -
meṉmai eṉiṉum, meṉkaṇam eṉiṉum, meli eṉiṉum melleḻutteṉṉum oru poruṭkiḷavi.	(mu.vī.eḻu. 17)
‘melloṟṟu valloṟṟiṟutik kiḷaioṟṟu ātal’ -
‘melloṟṟu iṟutivalloṟṟum kiḷaivalloṟṟum’ - eṉṟu pirittup poruḷ ceytaṉar iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. 	(to.e.415, 414)
	e-ṭu :kuraṅku+kāl= kurakkukkāl - melloṟṟu ṅakaram iṟuti valloṟṟātal. eṇku +kuṭṭi = eṭkuk kuṭṭi - melloṟṟu ṇakaram taṉ kiḷaiyāṉa ṭakara oṟṟātal.
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭut tantaṉar.
kiḷaiyoṟṟu eṉpatu avvav varukka oṟṟukkaḷai.
‘melloṟṟuk kiḷaivalloṟṟākum’- eṉṟa toṭarkku, melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa melloṟ ṟellām iṟutiyil niṟkum valloṟṟākum; aḵtāvatu iṟutiyil niṟkum valloṟṟāyt tiriyum eṉpatu.
	e-ṭu:	ōryāṇṭu + kuḻavi = ōryāṭṭaik kuḻavi
			kaṉṟu +tiraḷ = kaṟṟuttiraḷ
yāṇṭu, kaṉṟu - eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟiluḷḷa ṇakaraṉakaraṅkaḷ ṭakara ṟakaraṅkaḷākiya iṉaoṟṟukkaḷākat tirital kaṇṭu, anta ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷ piṟa eḻuttukkaḷoṭu kūṭi niṉṟavaḻiyum avvāṟu tiriyum eṉṟa karuti, eṇku+ kuṭṭi = eṭkuk kuṭṭi, eṉpu +kāṭu = eṟpukkāṭu - eṉap piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkiyamai tolkāppiyaṉārkku uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu. (e. ā. pak. 168. 169)
melittal, melippu -
valleḻuttai iṉamelleḻuttāka meypiṟitākkik kōṭal. aḵtāvatu valleḻuttai iṉamelleḻuttākat tirittal
makaraīṟṟup peyar vaṉkaṇam varumoḻi mutalil variṉ īṟṟu makaram keṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu iṭaiyē mikku,
maram +kiḷai =marakkiḷai - eṉṟāṟ pōla vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ varum. āṉāl iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tokaiyākap puṇarumpōtu, maram +kuṟaittāṉ =maraṅ kuṟaittāṉ - eṉa valleḻuttu mika vēṇṭiya iṭattu iṉamelleḻuttu varutal ‘melippoṭu tōṉṟal’ ākum. melippu - melleḻuttu.	(to.e.157 nac.)
meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇarcci -
alvaḻikkaṇ meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ vaṉkaṇam variṉum iyalpākap puṇarum.
e-ṭu :	kuraṅku kaṭitu, pañcu ciṟitu, vaṇṭutītu, pantu peritu,  naṇpu peritu, kaṉṟu peritu.
		1. 	melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum,  2. melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, 
3. melloṟṟut tiriyātu aikāramum val leḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
	e-ṭu :	1.	yāṇṭu  - ōryāṭṭai yāṉai, aiyāṭṭai erutu
		2.	aṉṟu, iṉṟu - aṟṟaik kūttar, iṟṟaip pōr
		3.	maṉṟu, paṇṭu	- maṉṟait  tūtu, maṉṟaip paṉai; 		paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr.       (to.e.425 nac. urai)
āṅku, īṅku, ūṅku, yāṅku, aṅku, iṅku, uṅku, eṅku - eṉpaṉa valloṟṟu mikku, āṅkuk koṇṭāṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōla muṭiyum. yāṅku eṉpatu yāṅkuk koṇṭāṉ - eṉa varutal perumpāṉmai; yāṅku koṇṭāṉ- eṉa iyalpāyum muṭiyum.	(nac . urai 427-429)
meṉtoṭarmoḻi vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉ val leḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu. e-ṭu: kuraṅkukkāl
iṭaiyē melloṟṟu valloṟṟāyt tirintu valleḻuttu mikutalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	: irupput toṭar, kurakkukkāl
			tattam iṉamāṉa valloṟṟākat tiritalumuṇṭu.
	e-ṭu :	eṇku+kuṭṭi =eṭkukkuṭṭi; eṉpu + kāṭu: eṟpukkāṭu; aṉpu + taḷai=aṟputtaḷai.
			paṟampiṟ pāri, kuṟumpiṟ cāṉṟōr - eṉa mel loṟṟut tiriyāmaiyum uṇṭu.
			iyalpu kaṇattilum kurakku ñāṟci, kurakku viral, kurakku (u)kir - eṉa melloṟṟu iṉavalloṟ ṟātalumuṇṭu.	(414 nac. urai)
meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukaraīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ (pullum aṭaṅkum) ammup peṟṟut teṅkaṅkāy,  cīḻkampul, kampam pulam, kamukaṅkāy eṉa varutalum koḷka. (teṅku, kamuku - ivaiyiraṇṭum pulliṉam.)	(415 nac. urai)
melloṟṟu valloṟṟu ākātu ammuccāriyai peṟum marappeyarkaḷum uḷa. e-ṭu: kuruntaṅ kōṭu, puṉkañ cetiḷ	(416 nac. urai)
akkuccāriyai peṟuvaṉavum uḷa. e-ṭu:kuṉṟakkūkai, maṉṟap peṇṇai 	(418 nac. urai)
melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyum marappeyarkaḷum koḷka. (pulliṉamum aṭaṅkum) e-ṭu: vēppaṅ kōṭu, īccaṅ kulai; (īñcu : pulliṉam)	(416 nac. urai)
nilaimoḻiyīṟṟil meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram niṟpa, varumoḻi mutaṟkaṇ vaṉkaṇam variṉum, alvaḻip puṇarcci yil iyalpāka muṭiyum.	(naṉ. 181)
	e-ṭu:	vantu kaṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ.
			ēḻām vēṟṟumaiiṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa aṅku iṅku uṅku eṅku āṅku īṅku ūṅku yāṅku āṇṭu īṇṭu yāṇṭu - eṉṉum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkaḷiṉ muṉ varum valliṉam mikum.
	e-ṭu:	aṅkuk kaṇṭāṉ, iṅkuc ceṉṟāṉ, āṇṭut tantāṉ, yāṇṭup pōṉāṉ.... mutalākak kāṇka.
ēḻām vēṟṟumaik kālapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa aṉṟu iṉṟu eṉṟu paṇṭu muntu- eṉṉum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu iṭaiccoṟkaḷiṉ muṉvarum valliṉam iyalpākavē muṭiyum.
	e-ṭu:	aṉṟu kaṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu ceṉṟāṉ, paṇṭu tantāṉ, muntu pōyiṉāṉ	(naṉ. 181 caṭakō. urai)
vēṟṟumaip puṇarccikkaṇ, meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu moḻikaḷuḷ cila nilaimoḻikaḷ (nāṟkaṇamvariṉum) tamakku iṉamākiya vaṉtoṭarkkuṟṟiyalukara moḻi kaḷākat tiriyum.
	e-ṭu:	maruntu +pai = maruttuppai; kuraṅku + maṉam =  kurakkumaṉam; irumpu + valimai = iruppuvalimai; kaṉṟu +ā =kaṟṟā (kaṉṟoṭu kūṭiya pacu)
ivai vēṟṟumaip puṇarcci. vēppaṅkāy eṉpatum atu; iṭaiyē ammuccāriyai mikkatu.
			nañcu + pakaimai=  naccup pakaimai (uvamattokai)
			irumpu +maṉam =iruppumaṉam (ituvumatu)
			eṉpu + uṭampu = eṟpuṭampu (irupeyaroṭṭu)
			ivai alvaḻip puṇarcci.
			kuraṅku +kuṭṭi =kuraṅkukkuṭṭi, kurakkukkuṭṭi (vēṟṟumai)
			aṉpu+taḷai =aṉputtaḷai, aṟputtaḷai (alvaḻi)	
iruvaḻiyum vikaṟpittu vantavāṟu.	(naṉ. 184)
aṉṟu, iṉṟu, (paṇṭu, muntu) aiyāṇṭu, mūvāṇṭu- eṉpaṉa aṟṟaip poḻutu, iṟṟai nāḷ (paṇṭaikkālam, muntai vaḷam) aiyāṭṭaip pirāyattāṉ, mūvāṭṭaik kuḻavi- eṉa aikāraccāriyai īṟṟil peṟṟup puṇarntaṉa. aṉṟu mutaliya nāṉkum melloṟṟu valloṟṟāyt tirintu aikārac cāriyai ēṟṟalum, paṇṭu- muntu- eṉpaṉa tiripiṉṟi aikāram ēṟṟalum kāṇka.	(naṉ. 185)
meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram -
ukaram ēṟiya valloṟṟukkaḷuḷ ( ku cu ṭu tu pu ṟu) oṉṟu moḻiyīṟṟatāy niṟpa ataṉ ayaleḻuttu oru melliṉa meyyāka variṉ, ammoḻiyiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramām.  kuṟṟiyalukarattukku māttirai arai.
	e-ṭu :	aṅku, pañcu, vaṇṭu, pantu, ampu, naṉṟu
			melloṟṟu āṟu ātaliṉ, ataṉai aṭuttu varum meṉtoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaramum āṟām.				(naṉ. 94)
meṉtoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam -
iruntu koṇṭu, kaṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ, ceṉṟu tantāṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōla ivvakaik kuṟṟiyalukaraīṟṟu viṉaiyeccam iyalpākap puṇarum.	(to. e. 427. nac.)
ēḻāvataṉ kālapporuḷavākiya coṟkaḷ iyalpākap puṇarvaṉa uḷa.
e-ṭu :	paṇṭu koṇṭāṉ, muntu koṇṭāṉ, iṉṟu			 koṇṭāṉ, aṉṟu koṇṭāṉ, eṉṟu koṇṭāṉ.	(430 iḷa.)
	uṇṭu eṉṟa col, uṇṭu poruḷ - uḷ poruḷ- eṉa iruvakaiyākap puṇarum.	(e. 430 nac.)
	uṇṭu kāṇam, uṇṭu cākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmarai,
	uṇṭu ñāṉam, uṇṭu yāḻ, uṇṭu āṭai - eṉa 		iyalpākap puṇarvaṉavē perumpāṉmai.				(430 nac. urai)
	vaṇṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉcāriyai peṟṟu		 vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	(420 nac.)
	peṇṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉcāriyai yōṭu 		aṉcāriyaiyum peṟṟup peṇṭiṉkai - peṇṭaṉkai - 	eṉa varum.	(421 nac.)
		oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku aintu - eṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷ aṉcāriyai peṟṟu oṉṟaṉ kāyam - eṉṟāṟ pōlap puṇarum. oṉṟaṉāṟ koṇṭa kāyam eṉa vēṟṟumaip puṇarcci (419 nac.)
meṉtoṭarmoḻip puṇarcci -
kuraṅku, kaḻañcu, eṇku, maruntu, pāmpu, eṉpaṉa- meṉ toṭarkkuṟṟukaraīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ. ivai vēṟṟumaip puṇarcciyil, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriya varu moḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; iyalpukaṇamāyiṉ melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirital oṉṟumē uṇṭu-
varumāṟu: kurakkukkāl, kurakkuccevi, kurakkuttalai, kurakkup puṟam - eṉavum, kurakkuñāṟci, kurakkuniṇam, kurakku mukam, kurakkuviral, kurakku (u) kir- eṉavum varum.
ñeṇṭu, pantu, paṟampu, kuṟumpu - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa mel loṟṟu valloṟṟu ākātaṉa; varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, iṉcāriyai ēṟṟu ataṉ ṉakaram ṟakara mākat tirintu muṭitalum ēṟṟa peṟṟiyākak koḷka.
varumāṟu: ñeṇṭukkāl, pantuttiraṭṭu; paṟampiṟ pāri, kuṟumpiṟ cāṉṟōr. 
‘maṉṉē’ eṉṟataṉāṉ. kurakkukkuṭṭi - kuraṅkiṉkāl, pāpput tōl- pāmpiṉ tōl- eṉa oṉṟu tāṉē ōriṭattut tirintum ōriṭattut tiriyātum varutalum koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, ’aṟputtaḷai’ (nālaṭi 12:2) aṉpiṉāṟ ceyta taḷai - eṉa vēṟṟumai yātalēyaṉṟi, aṉpākiya taḷai-eṉa alvaḻiyum ātaliṉ, alvaḻikkaṇṇum melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tirital koḷka.	(i.vi.eḻut. 103)
meṉtoṭar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ vaṉtoṭarāyt tirivaṉavum, tiriyātaṉavum, iraṇṭumāvaṉavum
kurakkukkāl, kaḻaccukkōl, eṭṭukkuṭṭi, maruttuppai, pāppukkāl eṟpuccaṭṭam - eṉat tirintaṉa. nāṟkaṇat toṭum oṭṭuka.
ñeṇṭukkāl, vaṇṭukkāl, pantut tiraṭci, kōṅkilai, icaṅkilai, puṉkaṅkāy, potumpiṟ pūvai, kuṟumpiṟ koṟṟaṉ - eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa tiriyāvām.
kurakkukkaṭi, kuraṅkiṉ kaṭi, vēppaṅkāy, vēmpiṉ kāy, pāpputtōl, pāmpiṉ tōl - eṉṟal toṭakkattaṉa tirintum tiriyātum vantaṉa.	(naṉ. 183 mayilai.)
moḻi iṭaip pōli -
moḻi iṭaikkaṇ akarattukku aikāram  ca ña ya - eṉṉum meykaḷukku muṉṉarp pōliyāka varum.
	e-ṭu :	aracu - araicu, ilañci - ilaiñci, arayar -araiyar.
aikārattil piṉṉarum yakarattiṉ piṉṉarum, ciṟupāṉmai yāka nakarattiṟku ñakaram pōliyāka varum.
	e-ṭu	:	mainniṉṟa - maiññiṉṟa; kainniṉṟa - kaiññiṉṟa; ceynniṉṟa - ceyññiṉṟa; neynniṉṟu - neyñ ñiṉṟu; ivai ceyyuḷ vaḻakku.
ulaka vaḻakkilum ainnūṟu, aiññūṟu, cēynalūr cēyñalūr eṉa varumāṟu kāṇaka.	(naṉ. 123, 124)
moḻi iṟutip pōli -
kuṟiliṇai aṭutta makaraīṟṟu aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu makarattukku ṉakaram pōliyāka varutaluṇṭu.
	e-ṭu	:	akam - akaṉ ; mukam -mukaṉ; nilam- nilam; kalam - kalaṉ 	(naṉ. 122)
iṉi, kuṟṟiyalukaraīṟṟup peyariṉ iṟuti ukarattukku ‘ar’ pōliyāka varutalum, īṟṟu lakarattukku rakaram pōliyāka varutalum, īṟṟu makarattukku lakaram pōliyāka varutalum, ciṟupāṉmai lakarattukku ḷakaram pōliyāka varutalum uraiyiṟ kōṭalām.
	e-ṭu	:	curumpu, curumpar, vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar ;
			pantal - pantar, cāmpal - cāmpar, kuṭal - kuṭar;
			tiṟam- tiṟal, pakkam - pakkal, kūvam - kūval;
			matil - matiḷ	(naṉ. caṭakōpa. urai)
moḻi iṟuti varum eḻuttukkaḷ -
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya  ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum meyyeḻuttup patiṉoṉṟum,  kuṟṟiyalukaramum moḻi yiṟutikkaṇ niṟkum eḻuttukkaḷām. āka avai irupattu nāṉku.	(naṉ. 107)
tāmēyum aḷapeṭuppuḻiyum meyyoṭu kūṭiyum varum uyirīṟu nūṟṟaṟupattu mūṉṟum, meyyīṟu patiṉoṉṟum, kuṟṟukara īṟu oṉṟum - ākap potuvakaiyāṉum ciṟappu vakaiyāṉum īṟu nūṟṟeḻupattaintum koḷka. avaṟṟuḷ utāraṇam kāṇātaṉa irupattiraṇṭu. avaiyum vantuḻik kāṇka.	(i.vi. 29 urai)
a ā i ī u ū ē ai ō - eṉṉum uyir oṉpatum, ṇakara makara ṉakaram eṉṉum melliṉam mūṉṟum, ya ra la va ḻa ḷa - eṉṉum iṭaiyiṉam āṟum moḻikku īṟām eḻuttukkaḷ (okaram nakarattoṭu kūṭi ‘no’ eṉa ivvoru colliṭattē māttiram īṟāka varum eṉpar uraiyāciriyar)	(nē. eḻut. 8)
uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ ekara okaram nīṅkalāṉa ēṉaiya pattum, melliṉa eḻuttukkaḷuḷ ṇakāra makāra ṉakāraṅkaḷākiya mūṉṟum, iṭaiyiṉa eḻuttukkaḷuḷ vakāraoṟṟu nīṅkalāṉa aintum moḻi iṟuti eḻuttukkaḷām.	(vī.cō. cantip. 8)
arukiyē moḻiyīṟāka varum ñakāra nakāra vakāra oṟṟukkaḷai viṭuttu ēṉaiyavaṟṟaiyē vīracōḻiyam kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ -
uyireḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakāra cakāra takāra nakāra pakāra makāra varukkaṅkaḷum, vakāra varukkattil u ū o ō- allāta eṭṭum, yakāra varukkattil  a ā u ū ō ōḷa- ākiya āṟum, ñakāra varukkattil a ā e ō - ākiya nāṉkum eṉṉum ivaiyaṉaittum tamiḻc collukku moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷām. āka, avai 12, 72, 8, 6,4 - eṉa 102 ām. 			(vī. cō. cantip.7)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉi raṇṭum, cakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, takara varukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, nakaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, pakaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum. makaravarukka uyirmeykaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum, vakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ eṭṭum, ñakara varukkattil mūṉṟum, yakara varukkattil mūṉṟum āka, ittoṇṇuṟṟeṭṭu eḻuttum moḻikku mutalām.
(vakaram a ā i ī e ē ai oḷa - eṉṉum eṭṭuyiroṭum, ñakaram ā e o - eṉṉum mūṉṟuyiroṭum, yakaram ā ū ō - eṉṉum mūṉṟuyiroṭum kūṭi moḻi mutalām eṉka.)			(nē. eḻut. 7)
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum, kakara cakara takara nakara pakara makara varukka uyirmeykaḷ taṉittaṉiyē paṉṉiraṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭāka eḻupattiraṇṭum, u ū o ō- allāta eṭṭu uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya vakaravarukka uyirmey eṭṭum, a ā u ū ō oḷa- eṉṉum āṟu uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya yakaravarukka uyirmey āṟum, a ā e o - eṉṉum nāṉku uyirkaḷoṭu kūṭiya ñakara varukka uyirmey nāṉkum, akara uyiroṉṟoṭum kūṭiya ṅakara uyirmey oṉṟum āka 103 eḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāvaṉa.	(naṉ. 102-106)
moḻikku mutalām eḻuttukkaḷ potuvum ciṟappumām iruvakaiyāṉum toṇṇuṟṟu nāṉkām eṉpatu aṟika. ‘cuṭṭuyā ekara....... mutalākummē’ (naṉ. 106) eṉṟārum uḷarālō eṉiṉ, mutalāvaṉa ivai īṟāvaṉa ivai eṉa īṇṭuk karuvi ceytatu mēl nilaimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutalōṭu iyaiyap puṇarkkumporuṭṭaṉṟē? avvāṟu puṇarttaṟku iyaipillāta ṅakaramum aṅṅaṉam - iṅṅaṉam - uṅṅaṉam- yāṅṅaṉam- eṅṅaṉam - eṉa ivvāṟu moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal payaṉil kūṟṟām eṉa maṟukka, aṉṟiyum, aṅku- āṅku, yāṇṭu - yāṇṭaiyatu, aṉṉa - eṉṉa eṉṟāṟpōlum ivvoṟṟukkaḷum moḻikku mutalām eṉṟal vēṇṭutalāṉ, avarkkum atu karuttaṉṟu eṉka.			(i.vi.27 urai)
moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ aṭṭavaṇai -
	tol	vīra	nēmi	naṉṉūl	ilakkaṇa	muttu	cuvāmi
